# Coaching Q&A: Mindset & Universal

## I need help sorting through self doubt

**Date:** 2025-06-18  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

There was a 2k for 2k Facebook community post by a member that talked about not being certified and struggling with feeling lost with knowing “how to coach”. I replied stating that I am in the exact same place. I have joined a certification program but it doesn’t “teach” anything. We have live calls each week where we practice, slowing down, leaving a lot of silence, asking questions that I find arbitrary or even nonsensical. I see the value in deep listening, holding space, slowing the conversation down and asking deep thought provoking questions. I have been struggling with whether or not to ask for a refund. Another member replied that there is a plan and roadmap and curriculum. I have been thinking about it ever since.

My thoughts: I am doing this all wrong. I have no business being a coach. If I coach someone, I am going to screw it all up and make a fool of myself. There is an educational path somewhere and I need to find it. My feelings: sad. defeated. hopeless. My actions: Ruminating, watching tv instead, try to convince myself that I can be a coach without this magical “plan, roadmap and curriculum”, do the dishes, avoid finding networking events, avoid sharing any of my experience and knowledge on social media, avoid sending any coaching session invitations like my coaching certification program encourages, search for another program that might finally be the one I have been looking for (the one that educates me on coaching). My results: No coaching clients.

I am a member of the Feminist Self Help Society with Kara Loewentheil. I am a member here and I am a member of the Ultimate Coaching program with the Insight Coaching Community.

Ultimately, I want to know what it means to be a great coach who can help people transform their lives after one coaching call. I am severely lacking the confidence that I have the knowledge to do that. I know Brooke Castillo will be offering some sort of training in the next few months and I desperately want to take part. I have no more money to invest in my coaching business, or me, until I get some clients.

Please help me sort through my thoughts and self doubt and create a plan. I need to know what baby steps to take each day to build my dream life. I am ready to face anything that comes up along the way to do that.

**ANSWER:**

You don’t feel confident coaching yet.

That’s where EVERYONE starts.

Or where they should start anyway.

It’s new, so of course you aren’t going to be great at it right away.

Think about anything else in life you’ve learned. Maybe it was a new job where you started out not knowing things and then learned and got good. Maybe it was in a sport. Or being a parent. Or learning how to drive a car for the first time.

You know you want to be a great coach and you’d like to come up with a plan for how to do that. You are already taking action and learning. You have learned more now than you knew a month ago.

With the training you are in, could you shift how you show up to learn more? For example, if you question why you are doing some of the things – do you feel like you could ask the instructors about it? It’s okay if you don’t feel like you could – but let’s explore how you might show up to get the most out of what you’ve already signed up for. That’s one step.

Another step is practicing coaching. It’s letting yourself be new and doing some coaching to learn what you know and where your gaps are now. Have you done some free coaching? How do you feel about doing some free coaching to get in some practice?

This coaching is meant to shift your mindset a bit – to help you see that where you are is normal and part of your journey. And to help your mind start to come up with a bit of a plan. Bring back your thoughts and what’s coming up for you reading this.

---

## Self doubt with social media marketing not getting the cut through I would like

**Date:** 2024-11-26  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

I would like to have more cut through on my marketing. I am just focusing on Instagram and emailing my list.

I have recently challenged myself to get out there and post/email regularly – photos, word slides, videos, free workbooks. I include hashtags on the socials.

Some videos have got 150 views. others 10.

No interactions – comments, followers, or link clicks.

Im proud I am getting myself out there.

Im regards to social media:

I am concerned I should be doing more but don’t know what it is.

That there is something I should be doing that creates more reach that I am not doing.

Am I missing something?

All creating self doubt and perhaps, now I think about it, the result is a scattergun approach??

Thank you.

**ANSWER:**

You want a strong presence on social media and you want people to see your posts, stop and watch them and share them with their friends.

You want more people seeing your posts.

If you look at your posts now, how might you have a stronger presence in those posts?

What thoughts and feelings are you having now when you are posting?

Vs. what thoughts and feelings do you think would create a strong presence?

---

## Day 3- Ask a coach Challenge: Self doubt showing up again! (Cont)

**Date:** 2024-10-21  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

You are exactly right! The all or nothing thinking is loud and clear! So here is my schedule last Tuesday.

6:30am-Wake up, make some tea, get my computer out

6:45-8:15am- Schedule podcast interviews, work on email sequence for new email subscribers

8:15-8:45am-Take kids to school

8:45-11am-Get myself ready for the day, tidy up the house, connect with my husband

11am-2:30pm- Record Podcast interviews with other coaches about style

2:30p-3:00pm- Lunch

3:00-3:30pm- Finish up recordings and get them filed for editing

3:30-4:30pm- Get the kids from school, connect with them about their day, connect with my husband

4:40-7pm- Back in my office working on my to do list, ie email sequence, stylist certification curriculum

7pm-8:30pm- Activity for my youth church group of which I’m a leader

8:30-10:30pm- Family time

10:30pm-Go to bed

This is a typical “work day” in my office and I can see a pretty good balance of work & family, so that is great news! I can see that my brain has lots of “but’s” when I see this all written out and it’s all about doing more in the time I have to get results faster, or expectations of what that work should be producing already, and me not being good enough (if I was doing it better I would have the results I want- that sort of thinking). This makes me feel frustrated and inadequate and those are pretty frequent emotions on the daily. SMH. Not the emotions I want hanging around all the time.

**ANSWER:**

You are definitely showing up and taking action.

Right now you are thinking things like “If I was doing better I would have more results” or “What I’m doing isn’t good enough.”

It’s SO easy to go down that path of thinking.

What we want to do instead is using the evaluation process. You can find this under Start Here –> Evaluating Your Results.

We want to evaluate what you are doing to see how you can be the most effective and what might need to shift. You also DO need to give things some time to work. It’s this mix of knowing you’ll create the results you want – because you are showing up and you are learning and evaluating as you go.

For example, on this day you spent time doing podcast interviews. We might look at, over time, how those are going. Are they creating clients for you? Why or why not?

A BIG part of evaluations is also looking at what IS working. You ARE showing up and taking action. You aren’t spinning or not doing anything all day. You will figure out how to create what you want by doing this schedule.

This is SO much more useful than thinking you aren’t doing enough or that you just need to work harder/faster.

## Day 3- Ask a coach Challenge: Self doubt showing up again!

**Date:** 2024-10-16  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

I asked myself why I haven’t made the money I wanted to make this month and a very familiar excuse is front and center. This is actually the reason I joined 2K, to get coached on THIS.

My family, being a mom, trying to build my business while being a mom. I’m having trouble figuring out a schedule to work on my business and being a mom.

I love being a mom and my kids are moving out and growing up so they are self sufficient in a lot of ways. I think I have time in my day and in my life that I’m just not seeing that I could use for my business but I’m just not seeing it. My youngest 2 kids are twins and they are 15. They are practicing their driving to get their licenses so I’m feeling like I’m just on the cusp of not running them around all the time. Because they are twins, everything a normal 15 year old is doing is doubled for me. I DO feel supported from my husband and he is completely willing to help when he can. I feel like if I knew what to ask him to help me with he would do it! I just don’t know what to ask him! I also know that I’m not managing the time I do have, well. I have a lot of garbage thoughts that are rooted in self doubt. I think that’s the problem truly.

If I get the “obstacle” solved and my family is completely sufficient on their own and don’t need me, what if I still don’t get the revenue result? That’s it! The self doubt is still there and I’m telling myself that the problem is that I don’t have the time. I’ve been watching and rewatching the belief in yourself videos but I guess there’s still something that isn’t clicking. There’s more work here for me. Can you give me some direction?

**ANSWER:**

Your mind is thinking that: “If I get the “obstacle” solved and my family is completely sufficient on their own and don’t need me, what if I still don’t get the revenue result?”

It’s very all or nothing. Either your kids need you and you have zero time to work on your business or your kids don’t need you at all. You know that your kids will always need you. You want to be there for them. It has and will change over the years, but it’s not that you will suddenly not be there for them at all or not spend time with them.

What we want to do is see how the priorities of your business and being a mom can work together. How can you do both really well?

Tell me about a typical Tuesday. Or last Tuesday. Let’s get the facts down. What did you do? Why did you decide to do each of those things? We want to get the facts of how you are spending your time so you can start being more intentional about it.

---

## Thought creation when confused and self doubt

**Date:** 2023-10-23  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

Ive been in doubt about both my coaching training and also who I coach (which is what I’e been trained on. My last question is about it too. How can I decry between inner knowing on where my passion is, to avoidance, fear, and drama? How can I create certainty (fee free to direct me to a model too if helpful).

Ive been trained in coaching couples but suspect I prefer to coach women on their relationship, and now feel I need more training..I know its a common pitfall

C – doubt and confusion about my coaching ability and best clients

T- I need another training

F- perfetionsm, control

A- checking out a lot of modalities and free trainings, looking for the right modality, not going deeper into what I’ve already studied, consuming more than producing. My content a little all over the place

R- not booking clients

C- doubt and confusion

T- I can help, people need it

F- commitment

A- meet people tell them im a relationship coach and I an help, do more social media

R – book consult

Im not sure I’m doing this right…

Thank you for your insight

**ANSWER:**

You’re doing this perfectly, coach – since you’re showing up and sharing what’s coming up for you. Well done.

Yes – thinking we need more trainings focuses on lack, and blinds us to the powerful skills which we already possess to help those around us.

And certainly, there is a time for education, whenever there is a specific skill which is required in order to reach/help the people we are in business to serve. Based on what you’ve shared so far, we don’t know whether that’s the case here.

So let’s find out – why do you think you need another training to help women in relationships, specifically?

Focus on your body for a moment. How does your body feel when you think, “I can help, people need it?”

What are the specific ways you can help women with their relationships?

* How do you feel when you think about helping them with those things?

* How eager are you to serve them?

## Allowing versus Resisting Self Doubt

**Date:** 2023-04-18  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

The past few days I have been feeling self doubt. Doubting myself, what I have to offer, if what I selling is truly valuable.

I have been listening to Making Offers BootCamp and In week 4 Stacey talks about allowing self doubt versus resisting it. I think I have been resisting it. Hoping for it to go away fast so I can feel better and be in a better place to make offers, and hoping to get rid of the thoughts that are leading to self doubt. How do I allow self doubt and still show up to make offers and give value?

**ANSWER:**

I want you to answer this question first.

“How do I allow self doubt and still show up to make offers and give value?”

Let’s imagine I’m a client and you are helping me lose weight or find someone to marry. I have lots of self doubt that I can do it. How do I eat healthy and exercise anyway? How do I go on dates anyway?

## Day 4: Self Doubt

**Date:** 2023-02-22  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

“Me: Why put in the effort if no one will see it, or if they get the same kind of information from other sources that know more or present the ideas in a much better way? Why me?

Coach: You will be able to say it in a way that resonates with YOUR people. Other coaches will say it in a way that resonates with THEIR people. This is how we attract the people we’re meant to serve. What comes up for you with that? You asked, “Why me?” Yes, answer that. Why you?”

Why me:

–I have been through the same struggles as my clients and have been able to get through.

–My combination of life experiences, years of research, introspection, and deep thinking about clients gives me a unique perspective that many people may never have heard before.

–I might be the only life coach that some of my audience is following; they may not be hearing any of this anywhere.

–I believe this is something the universe is leading me to do; there is some greater purpose to why I am doing this.

–There are at least a couple people following me that really trust me and my advice/opinions/ideas/perspective. They have shared with me that they are inspired by what I share, and it helps them. They see me differently than I see me.

–I have done big scary things in my life, taken risks, achieved major goals, done things I never thought I could or would ever do, and jumped into things knowing I would figure it out. If I can do that with all the fear I have around doing new things and around change, then I know ANYONE can do that, and this belief that I have in people’s ability to dive in head first and figure it out can really serve those that follow me – I THINK THIS IS THE MOST MOTIVATING “WHY ME” THAT I LISTED – remembering all of the big scary moments I’ve chosen to go through to get what I wanted in life brings me a lot more confidence. I almost stopped before thinking of this one because I thought my brain was done

Right now, I have major attachment to the outcome of signing clients, but if I release that attachment, it feels lighter, less stressful, and more fun.

I think I really need to work on showing up as a coach everywhere I go, like Stacey talks about. I think I have lost the meaning of what embodying a life coach is, like I don’t know what it feels like anymore, like I don’t know how to do it anymore.

What does showing up in life coach energy really mean?

How can I begin to see myself like others see me (at least the people that like what I share!)?

**ANSWER:**

Awesome work here!

These answers reflect your truth. It’s what you know to be true about yourself.

How do you feel when you think about these things you listed?

This is the energy that you want to show up in as much as possible.

And you do that by referring back to this list as often as needed in order to get you there.

What comes up for you with that?

---

## Day 3: Self Doubt

**Date:** 2023-02-17  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

“You get to simply remind yourself ‘Of course it feels like too much or too hard or I’m too tired. That’s ok, but we’re going to do it anyway…for our future self who already has the result and for the people who need us to show up. It’s bigger than us.’

What comes up for you with that?”

What’s coming up is that telling myself that makes perfect sense, I love the idea of thinking of future me and what my clients need. It’s super helpful, but it’s still not exactly leading to action in business. It leads to ideas and planning but not executing. I need to pay attention to the feeling I have that leads to that kind of action because I HAVE had that type of motivation before.

And I have a strong feeling there is a sneaky thought in there somewhere about how people can get the info/perspective I share from other people, and other people will say and present it better than me anyway. And that leads to a “what’s the point?” kind of thought. Why put in the effort if no one will see it, or if they get the same kind of information from other sources that know more or present the ideas in a much better way? Why me? I think when I try to answer this question, I don’t go deep enough or maybe don’t believe my answers

**ANSWER:**

This is good.

For sure people can get the info/perspective you share from other people.

However, no one else will present it the same way as you. With your personality, your energy, and your words. All based on your own unique experience that no one else has.

You will be able to say it in a way that resonates with YOUR people. Other coaches will say it in a way that resonates with THEIR people. This is how we attract the people we’re meant to serve.

What comes up for you with that?

You asked, “Why me?”

Yes, answer that. Why you?

---

You get to simply remind yourself “Of course it feels like too much or too hard or I’m too tired. That’s ok, but we’re going to do it anyway…for our future self who already has the result and for the people who need us to show up. It’s bigger than us.”

What comes up for you with that?

## Day 1: Spinning in & paralyzed from self doubt

**Date:** 2023-02-15  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

I am committing to daily coaching for the next 20 weekdays

Question: how can I get out of this self-doubt trap??

I have had 2 clients in the last year, one of which re-signed for a 2nd 6-month package which will be up in 2.5 months. I am in my 5th year of my coaching business and am feeling more and more doubt in my capabilities of having a successful (5+ paying clients at a time) coaching business. The most clients I’ve worked with at a time was 5, but only one was paid, the rest were not.

C:

2 clients over the last year and one consult that was a “not right now,” 5th year of business

T:

*I’m never going to figure this out

*Nothing I do makes a difference

*How can I coach on goals if I can’t reach my business goals?

*I’m a failure

*Nothing is working

*I don’t know what to do anymore

*This used to be fun and exciting, but it’s not anymore

F:

Paralyzed

Numb

Ashamed

Depressed

Stressed

A:

*Post a couple times a week on IG

*Write out ideas but never do anything with them

*Make my action plan then don’t follow through

*Coach my current client but not over delivering even though she’s my only client right now

*Stress about only 1 consult in several months

*Stress about no coaching income after current client package is over

*Get sick from the stress and can’t show up

R:

No consults, no clients, no one to make offers to aside from on instagram

I REALLY want to completely shift my self concept around being capable of the kind of hard work that owning a business comes with and showing up in a much bigger way.

How can I get unstuck?

**ANSWER:**

Hey Coach!

First, it makes total sense that your results are what they are when this is the energy you’re using to fuel what you do in your business.

It doesn’t matter how much action you take, it won’t create the results you want from this energy.

Also, remember that what you focus on, you create more of. Your thought will always show up somewhere in your result.

So as long as you’re focusing on “I’m never going to figure this out”, “Nothing I do makes a difference”, you’ll create more of that. And you’ll stay stuck here.

Now, you’re using your past results as an indicator of what’s possible for you.

Do you want to do that? Why or why not?

So check in with yourself for a minute. What do you KNOW to be true about yourself and who you are when it comes to your business?

What comes up for you when you read that again to yourself?

Bring that back here for further coaching!

---

## I think this is about self doubt… but it could fit in many places

**Date:** 2022-10-06  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

Hi,

I am not sure where this fits in the 2k process, but I’m pretty sure it’s about the belief triad.

After saying “yes” to my 6 month program, my client (we hadn’t started yet) emailed with concern about doing the full 6 months – said she was worried about the money and would I consider a 3 month program. This came AFTER she had agreed – a reflection of uncovered objections. I am a fairly new coach. I wish I had suggested we hop on a call to talk about it. Instead, I emailed her the reasons why I do a 6 month instead of 3, and said however, “I totally understand” her concern (I know I got into the pool with her) and “how about this”… I proposed we start AS IF we are doing the full 6, and then at 3 months we will reevaluate, with her having the option of stopping.

I know now that I never want to do that again. Why? I feel like I gave her a total out from following through with her own desires to make changes, to make mistakes, and to still have the time to get back to herself. So now – here’s where we are… we are approaching the 1/2 way mark. I sent her a halfway revisit form and self evaluation – something I’ve created to help me and the client make adjustments moving forward in the 2nd half. Within the email I reminded her that we are coming up to the 1/2 way mark and that we can discuss the plan at our next appointment. She has asked to reschedule and now hasn’t gotten back to me.

I have a lot of mind drama about it. I find myself wanting to “convince” her to stay with the program. I also have noticed she’s been falling away from taking action – specifically making the schedule shifts that she has been saying she wants to make. I can’t make her do it. And my brain is having thoughts like, “she has already decided to stop, and she’s not putting the effort in anymore”.

I am not sure where to go from here. I want to show up as a coach – to coach her through the decision without convincing energy. And I’m not sure how to do it without that, or without throwing in the towel on her. Also – I may have just set this up all wrong and I realize I may have to say, “Okay – lesson learned – I won’t do that again.”

Any help to organize my thoughts and move forward with a plan would be greatly appreciated!!

**ANSWER:**

This is good, Coach. Every decision we make in our business is an opportunity to learn. So let’s take a look.

Why do you think you agreed to this even though you believe 6 months best serves your clients?

How do you feel about that reason?

Now, you shared, “I find myself wanting to “convince” her to stay with the program.”

Because if she stays in the program, what will you get to believe about yourself?

Why don’t you believe that now?

How will it serve her to stay in the program?

---

## Self Doubt Part Three

**Date:** 2022-09-16  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

AAC: What do you think?

How easy will it be for you to not judge yourself, and instead meet yourself with curiosity to examine how you can improve for next time?

I think I can have more compassion for myself and my coaching, and focus more on the evidence that it is brilliant. I find myself shaming myself (I should have said this or that) over and over again, and this is on top of the insecurity and probably fuels it. So realizing that feels a bit like a release because now being aware of it I can choose to stop doing that. And I can be compassionate toward myself in terms of growing as a coach, and use self-evaluations to watch myself evolve instead of beating myself up. I also have this thought “I should not be judging myself” so shame on the shame. So crazy.

**ANSWER:**

In your last submission you stated that you want to spend more time celebrating what is going well instead of dwelling on what isn’t going well.

Let’s do that.

What is going well? What is working?

Along a similar line…

How CAN you help people?

What DO you know?

## Self doubt

**Date:** 2022-08-24  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

Answer

You shared that you have had a hard time gaining more clients. Let’s take a look at that before you make a decision about if you want to work as a contracted coach or for yourself.

Why do you think you have had a hard time creating clients?

How have you created clients so far? How have you met them, told them you are a Coach and make them an offer?

MY ANSWER:

Why have I had hard time creating clients:

I personally have been at times overwhelmed with being a new mother. My schedule has always been my own and not having the same amount and freedom of time has been hard. I have been a lot more up and down than usual. Out of that state my belief in myself and my value has been lower. I haven’t networked as much and haven’t been putting the same time in social media value posts (I am working on that. I have done self coaching and started separating my skill as a coach- which is always there- from my current situation and tried to use that as an advantage (ie life can happen, but you can still have clarity, peace of mind etc and this is how) and asking myself what is my value, what can I offer, what can I offer more of now that I am in this situation and how is it true can I ALWAYS be a great coach no matter what I personally go through))

How I created clients in the past: my belief was high and I was exposed to a lot of people through my full time job, social life and additional networking. I was constantly making value posts and telling others I am a coach.

I would very much love to just work for myself rather than be a contractor. Here is my plan of action so far (please feel free to add what you think fit)

Regularly do thought work on what I have to offer ( I also noticed myself consuming more content to be more “qualified to coach”-always my hiding method. Lately- before watching another webinar on a subject I make myself sit down and write what I already know about- what value can I already share before consuming more info”). What I already know. What tools I already have. What results I have helped people achieve. What people get from working with me.

REvive my social life. I started networking again and meet 2-3 people a week for 1/1 calls online. I would like to start networking locally- even just for my own sanity and mental well being. It will naturally lead to conversations about what I do etc etc.

would love any additional feedback!

**ANSWER:**

It totally makes sense that there would be an adjustment period when you become a new mother. Of course things are going to have to shift and you will have to find new ways of doing things. Of course how you spend your time will change.

And it sounds like these are some great ideas on how to be the mom you want to be and have the business that you want. You can keep trying things out give yourself time to let yourself grow into this new you who is a mom AND a Coach.

---

## Self doubt/making decisions

**Date:** 2022-08-22  
**Topics:** self doubt

**QUESTION:**

I am currently on mat leave from my full time job. I was making around $2k/mo from my coaching practice before mat leave- plus my full time income. I would like to have a financial option to continue staying home with my child when my mat leave is up- but have had hard time gaining more clients. One of the options I have considered is completing LCS certification then working for them as a contractor and coach for the scholars program. It would provide me enough income to stay home, but it is a big financial investment in a coaching skill when I am already a certified coach. Plus the minimum requirement to coach for them is 20 hours, which means I won’t have a lot of time to coach my own clients/ develop my own practice. I like the guarantee of income of that option, but notice that when I think of that it means that I don’t believe in myself. That I don’t believe in my ability to create 10 or so consistent clients in the next 7 months, which is disappointing to me, because deep inside I know I am capable of that.

I am a little lost on what decision to make- to take the training or not. I do want to do it at some point- because I always love to learn and uplevel myself, but I don’t want to do as a a way of procrastinating. I am fearful that I make a decision to go all in and believe in myself- not take the training- then not having to created the needed income. I am recognizing that my brain is full of fear and scarcity and I want to get out of it.

**ANSWER:**

You shared that you have had a hard time gaining more clients. Let’s take a look at that before you make a decision about if you want to work as a contracted coach or for yourself.

Why do you think you have had a hard time creating clients?

How have you created clients so far? How have you met them, told them you are a Coach and make them an offer?

I have had multiple clients and even while I was going through my own personal things my clients were saying they were having huge transformations in their lives from our coaching together but now I am pausing my business and stuck in my own head.

I think I have 3 models here:

T: I don’t know whether to stay or leave

my marriage

F: confused/stuck

A: do nothing and stay in marriage

R: nothing changes and I stay confused and stagnant

T: I can’t grow a coaching business while being married to an alcoholic (like people will judge)/I can’t be who I want to be while being with an alcoholic

F: frustrated

A: don’t put myself out there

R: business does not grow

T: I can’t grow my business until I have my own life figured out

F: impatient

A: keep learning new things to distract myself

R: don’t take action growing my business

doing hard things and coaching myself to make a decision.

Confusion is my big indulgent emotion , unfortunately.

What do you think people will say/think?

Why can’t you be who you want to be while being with an alcoholic?

## Confidence without evidence

**Date:** 2026-02-26  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

I am a business mindset coach for coaches and service providers. I am also a therapist so I call myself a business therapist and I help my clients with their confidence fractures that they experience after a set back in life or a slow season in their business. I tell my clients that once their confidence and conviction get back on track they will start to sign clients again consistently. My problem is I am not signing clients consistently and I think it’s because I don’t feel confident that I can help my clients sign clients. So, I feel I’m in a bit of a pickle. LOL.

**ANSWER:**

Hey coach,

I’m really glad you’re being honest about this, because this is exactly the kind of thing that quietly affects results without us realizing it.

You’re right, if part of you doesn’t fully believe you can help your clients sign clients, that will interfere with you signing clients. Not because anything has gone wrong with your offer, but because your belief drives how you show up. This is the belief trail at work.

So I want to start here: 
what is creating the doubt for you right now?
I’m asking because confidence doesn’t just disappear on its own. It always comes from a thought. Something has led you to question whether you can help people get results. What specifically are you thinking that makes you feel less certain?

I also want to clarify what “consistently signing clients” means to you. Are you signing clients, just not at the frequency you want? Or have you stopped signing them altogether? This matters because your brain may be interpreting a slower season as “this isn’t working,” which then weakens your conviction, even though nothing about your ability has actually changed.

Another question I want you to consider is: 
what are your top three reasons you believe you’re not signing clients right now?
I’m asking this because the answers will show us what you’re currently believing. And those beliefs not your skill or your offer are what are shaping your results.

Confidence is not something that comes from clients signing. Confidence is something you choose first. If you’re waiting for clients to sign in order to feel confident, you’ll stay stuck in that loop. It’s like waiting to feel ready before you take action. The confidence comes from deciding you believe in your work and continuing to show up.

Confidence is an inside job.

Nothing has gone wrong here. Your brain is responding to your current results and offering doubt. But you don’t have to accept that doubt as truth. You can look at it, understand what’s creating it, and decide what you want to believe instead.

The most important thing is cleaning up your belief in your ability to help people. When that belief is clean, your actions naturally follow.

Share what is coming up for you as I mention your belief is impacting your selling .

## Creating Confidence & Certainty

**Date:** 2025-11-06  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

I recently went through a rebrand and shifting from mostly being a health & life coach to fully being a life & identity coach. I love this shift and I’ve had all the downloads coming in that felt right & where I’m going, my evolution & growth as a coach. And, it also has me feeling a little wobbly with my confidence and certainty because it’s not the same thing or me being the same version of myself I’ve been the last 4 years. I’ve noticed this then of course affects me showing up as powerfully to market & sell…and I see and want to call in a higher caliber client.

What can I do to help me anchor in and create obscene confidence (obscene is one of my new words I am LOVING) & certainty??

My guesses/ what has come up for me:

I feel like what would help is I don’t/haven’t fully written out all of my new intellectual property and pillars of my new life and identity coaching. Like my methods to help someone. I’ve definitely done it, 1,000’s of times, but I never claimed owned or stopped to tether out the how to rinse and repeat it…I had that for my health coaching. But my brain is also like, how do I do this for life coaching/for stepping into your new identity. Creating a process/steps my brain resists and wants to do anything but this. To mine for what I’ve done to create my transformation & results and then make it a process.

I also feel like I have certainty, I really do KNOW I can help and powerful my coaching is, and yet there is another side that is like, waiting to be found out. This really is silly because I have coached 1000’s in my day job as a coach & in my business I’ve had over 10 1:1 clients & over 7 in a group. 2. You think that taking more people through that process would help too. It might help you refine it and really see the impact of it. You’ve already taken some people through it and taking even more people through it can help you deepen that certainty.

## Increased confidence

**Topics:** confidence

My confidence level at being a life coach wavers back and forth. I would like to increase it along with meet more people and tell them I am a life coach. I am ending the school year as a school counselor and would like to dedicate more time into my business. While I will be working as a therapist starting the second week of June to secure income, I want to spend more time in my business and get clients. I signed up for two networking events this week, and will do a workshop series for my children’s daycare employees next week which I am excited about. The problem that I have is when my current colleagues ask me what is next, I tell them that I will work as a life coach and I feel like there are crickets after my statement or people will say ooh I might need to hire you or I need a life coach. What led you to coaching vs. another career path? Why do you do this work vs. being a nurse or an accountant?

YES, I would love to focus on my confidence in selling. I’ve identified another area I’m struggling and I’d love to hear your thoughts on if they’re connected (I think they are, because I think the gap in my selling is my confidence and belief in ME and what I have to offer.)

So this other place where I need help: I have lots of creative ideas for how to meet people. But I never act on them. In 4 years of growing my willingness to get out of my house to meet people, I’ve definitely made progress but still don’t have that leadership/ initiative to act on my creative ideas.

And I suppose that’s connected to selling because, for instance, one of the things I want to do is host free meetups and eventually host paid live events, and I understand that, even with a free event, I have to sell and market and tell people about it. And if I were more confident in my ability to do that it would definitely help me get over my fear and overwhelm.

So yeah, let’s do this! And to answer your specific questions from the previous coaching:

– I think I discount my knowing, yes, because I focus more on what I lack than on all the successful times I’ve sold 1:1 coaching, a small group previously, attendance to free courses, and more. I diminish it and have a lot of “yeah buts” and ”that didn’t count because”

– The gap in my selling skills now is connecting to people ( I think). No one knows I’m here. And I also don’t think I’m super sold on the value of what I’m offering.

Let’s start the offer part of this by looking at what you can hep with now. You will be a better coach 10 years from now than you are today. That’s the goal – is to keep getting better. ## Follow up #3 to Losing Confidence – No consults

**Date:** 2025-02-25  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

Previous coaching included building my belief in myself, my why and my coaching, which helped me see that I have a strong why and I believe that my coaching goes beyond just weight loss. Here was the second part the coach said: “Let’s get back to some of that belief that you have in yourself and in what you do. Let’s start with that, then we can look at some actions you want to be taking each week and how you CAN meet people who want your help.”

I have thoughts that people want an easy fix to weight loss. They don’t respond to “live your life free from food drama” or the idea that your thoughts can change your reality. They are uncomfortable and are afraid if they go on a journey it will be work and that work may not pay off in the end. I do want to be patient. I have stated in the previous coaching all my time constraints and I do want to start pulling away from the 9-5 and taking the small actions. I want to meet more people that want my help – i know i’m not for everyone and that is OK. I just have been in my practice in the pediatrician role for so long that it is a strong identity. It’s an identity that I don’t necessarily need to want to abandon as it has shaped my coaching style and what I can offer to my clients as far as experience and how I relate to them. However, people do have a hard time seeing an M.D. who now is seemingly downgrading to coaching. I see it as a TOTAL upgrade. The level of service to the other person, the results that can be created for someone’s life is priceless. I just think I feel maybe not good at explaining it? I have shame about leaving or wanting to leave my career? I haven’t figured out where to meet the people – I’m being honest and I really have not much of an idea about this- since weight loss can be shameful for the client to seek help for. Also some people see me as an “authority” or someone they feel ashamed to “report to” even though I’m not that type of coach. I just need some guidance on where to start putting my energy to get consults – I have not had a consult in like 4 months at least. I feel like I should be doing at least one consult every 1-2 weeks

**ANSWER:**

You want to start with figuring out how to see people.

You don’t have to be patient. You can start creating clients this week. There are people looking for help with weight loss right now.

Your mind is telling you that those people looking for weight loss want a quick fix and want to take medication.

Tell me why you do weight loss without medication. Could I just take medication to lose weight? Will that work? Or are there reasons you teach weight loss without medication?  This might be what you talk about with people. If you think that lots of people are considering weight loss medication – then that can be a great way to start talking about it with them.

Do you ever see clients as an MD who don’t want to take medication? Do you think there are people who want to lose weight but don’t want to take medication for it?

We are looking at the answers to these questions because your mind has to know that clients exist for you before you can see where to meet them. If I tell you that you can meet these people online, in FB groups, on Instagram, at in person networking events, through friends/family, you still won’t think that you know how to meet people if you don’t believe people want to lose weight without medication. We need to build evidence that people want to lose weight without medication. You have to start making your mind see how that’s true.

Let’s talk about specific meeting people actions:

“I haven’t figured out where to meet the people – I’m being honest and I really have not much of an idea about this- since weight loss can be shameful for the client to seek help for.”

Where could you meet people? I’ll give you some ideas but add on what you have done too. Remember you don’t have to do all of these, but this gives you some ideas for where to start.

Doing a talk at local gyms on nutrition/weight loss

Using Instagram or Facebook or LinkedIn

Can you let patients know that you also coach on weight loss in your practice? (I’m not sure about the ethics around that, but that might be a possibility.)

Going to networking events

Telling people what you do as a coach and talking about it proudly. For example, when the person asked if you could make money as a coach opening up a conversation about the clients you’ve had and the results you’ve helped clients with

Let’s keep working through this.

Looking at that list of actions – what actions do you want to start taking this week in the limited time you do have?

## Follow up to Losing Confidence- no consults

**Date:** 2025-02-19  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

Answer to first part from coach: Let’s get back to some of that belief that you have in yourself and in what you do. Let’s start with that, then we can look at some actions you want to be taking each week and how you CAN meet people who want your help.

People do want help with weight loss. How is that true?

Your background and coaching knowledge is how you’ll help people lose weight. What have you taught those 5 clients about weight loss that helped them succeed?

Why do you want to be a coach instead of staying in medicine?

My Answers: 1. People do want help with weight loss. It IS true. This is everywhere. Seems like everyone is on some sort of ozempic, advertising is through the roof for these at home meds too with follow up coaching support. I have found that my thought is people want a quick fix and they are thinking if I’m a doctor, why the hell am I not prescribing this medication and coaching at the same time. I know my people are out there who know they can do this without relying on medication in the long term but that takes work and I think it is hard to find the people that need me and want me and also to get them to really believe this is possible with all the messaging that this is not possible without medications or out of reach.

2. I have taught my clients so much. I just finished a testimonial google form that I plan to have those that have finished 6 months with me to give me the feedback from their perspective. I do have a client who is struggling and has really not lost any weight in the past 4 months, but I’m making that mean I’m not a good coach so i’m working on that too coaching myself. I have taught them about hormones, about insulin, how to manage their thoughts and the thought model, and all the aspects that go into achieving and maintaining healthy weight. I had 3 clients re-sign with me for continuation. 2 signed for another 6 months, and 1 for another year!

3. I want to be a coach instead of staying in medicine because I want to deeply impact lives and I see myself actually impacting a generation of women and having that ripple versus the work I do in clinic which I do impact lives, but it is very superficial. It doesn’t give me purposely more.

This week I did talk to 2 of the moms in clinic about what I do and it didn’t feel weird. I want to work on what you said about what actions can I take to meet people who want to work with me and get the word out in my community where I am known and respected already.

**ANSWER:**

You know people want help with weight loss. Why is it important for some people to do it without medication? What’s your stance on medication vs. no medication?

Answering that will really help you in your marketing too.

There will sometimes be clients who don’t get results or who take longer to get results. That will happen. Those clients are the ones you’ll learn the most from. How has that person grown even if they haven’t lost the weight? Has the coaching impacted them in other ways?

You have such a strong WHY for going into coaching. You know the impact it can have on lives. And the ripple effect. You want to remind yourself of that when things get hard or when people have doubts. You know why you are doing this coaching work.

You shared a woman you met asked if you could really make money as a coach. You have made money. You made 21k last year while still working in medicine too! You are an example that it’s possible.

How would you respond to her now after going through this coaching here?

---

## No consults – losing confidence

**Date:** 2025-02-18  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

I have been coaching for 2 years. I am an MD (23 years experience) and a certifiedhealth and wellness coach, i am also almost a certified life coach currently in a training program. Last year I made around 21k doing 1:1 coaching. I coach professional women on weight loss without medications. I have had success with my clients (but there are only 5 of them. I love sending newsletters with inspiration and stories, I post on social maybe once or twice a week. I work almost full time in my MD job and I went through a tragic and difficult time recently. In August 2024 my elderly parents were in a near fatal accident. Long story but my father passed in October and I am my mother’s only child -I’m helping her a couple of days a week as far as appointments, and errands and I have taken over her financials. I also have 2 teenage boys at home. I love coaching. My goal is to leave medicine entirely and become a full time coach. I am trying to get out and meet new people. I went through the whole 2k process and I recently started watching the videos again. I have gone out to coffees with friends I haven’t seen in a while and also a couple of new people I have met in the past 1-2 years. I am on the physician mom’s groups on Facebook but I don’t find where I can add value there sometimes it’s full of political rant and I did reach out to one physician who was looking for help with weight loss and asked outright to the group.

I had a recent experience that made me want to reach out and write this: I was out at dinner with my husband’s clients. I sat next to a woman who is an esthetician – I was really interested in her work and we had a lovely conversation about many topics. Then she asked me “What do you do for work?” and when I said I was an MD turning life coach her face dropped. She said “can you make any money doing that?” I felt shame for wanting to leave pediatrics even though I know I can make more as a life coach. It was like my parents were there telling me that I was out of my mind trying to be someone else after I trained and devoted my life to medicine. The doubt came too – I don’t know what I’m doing. There are so many MD coaches that are better than me and know more than me and I’m trying to cobble together this little business while I need to pay for my boy’s college and keep our household running. I don’t want to give up but I feel overwhelmed. Not a single consult in months so I feel no one wants what I do.

**ANSWER:**

There are other coaches who were doctors or lawyers or other professions where they did a lot of training and made lots of money – who are now coaches making lots of money. It IS possible.

Right now you have a lot of other things you are focused on. You are working. You are raising two teenagers. You are helping out your parents. And you are feeling doubt – you have had some clients but it feels like it’s hard to book consults. It’s hard to know that people are interested.

Let’s get back to some of that belief that you have in yourself and in what you do. Let’s start with that, then we can look at some actions you want to be taking each week and how you CAN meet people who want your help.

People do want help with weight loss. How is that true?

Your background and coaching knowledge is how you’ll help people lose weight. What have you taught those 5 clients about weight loss that helped them succeed?

Why do you want to be a coach instead of staying in medicine?

## Building Confidence in Making Offers: Overcoming Challenges and Staying Consistent

**Date:** 2025-01-28  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

Evaluation: Tuesday, January 28th**

**What Went Well:**

– I had a meaningful conversation with the new receptionist, Che, and enjoyed connecting with her. The Wi-Fi being down encouraged more conversations among coworkers.

– I made one offer to help and allowed myself to notice and process feelings of awkwardness and rejection without turning them into problems.

– I printed flyers, displayed one at work, and casually announced my free offer.

– I engaged in a lovely online conversation with Olivia.

**What Didn’t Go Well:**

– I missed making four offers to help.

– I didn’t create an Instagram post or consider alternatives.

– I didn’t submit an Ask a Coach question.

– On my one offer to help, the person made a sarcastic remark about me being able to change his life in one month and laughed. It seemed confronting for him, and he became defensive. I accepted this without letting it affect me.

**What I Will Do Differently:**

1. **Schedule Ask a Coach Submission:**

– Dedicate 10 minutes at the start of my day to write and submit a question. If needed, I can prepare a draft or simply use a thought download or unintentional model. Even this evaluation can serve as my submission.

2. **Streamline Instagram Posting:**

– Schedule 20 minutes at the start of my day to create and post on Instagram. To streamline this process, I’ll:

– Maintain a folder of ready-to-go posts.

– Prepare a folder of images.

– Keep a list of ideas for captions and content.

3. **Intentional Networking in the Real World:**

– Schedule 2 hours after journaling and book writing to focus solely on networking. This includes:

– Talking to people in the gym in areas like the steam room, bath, or bar.

– Using questions like the magic wand question to lead into offers of help.

– Visiting local businesses and events to connect and distribute flyers.

4. **Dedicated Writing Time:**

– Schedule 1 hour daily for writing my book.

Question

How can I stay consistent with offering help, especially when I encounter defensive reactions from others? I want to keep my energy high and not let fear of rejection hold me back from making offers.

**ANSWER:**

Great job evaluating!

You can definitely use evaluations to submit to Ask A Coach.

Let’s look at that person’s response to you. He said something sarcastic about being able to change is life in a month.

What did you think when he said that?

Why do you think he made the comment he did – what do you think was coming up for him?

Let’s work through this so responses like this don’t affect your energy or you showing up. Let’s come up with a plan to know how you want to respond.

---

## Response to my previous Ask A Coach “Workshop Planning and Skill & Confidence & Momentum Development – (91) (93) (96)PART2” – (99)

**Date:** 2025-01-06  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

Thanks for your support. My response to your questions are after >>>

Answer

When you are inviting people to the workshop – you want to be thinking about how the workshop will benefit them and how it will help them.

Right now you are thinking that you don’t want people to think you are using them or meetup. But what happens when instead you are thinking about how valuable that workshop can be and wanting to let people know about it so they can come?

Let’s practice that for your next workshop. How can it really help someone? Who should for sure come?

Really knowing that people WANT to come to the workshop is going to have you showing up differently. Instead of feeling sneaky – you can enthusiastically tell people about the workshop. Because they want to come. What you are sharing is so valuable. It really is. What do you think about that?

>>> A belief that came up while I was working with my coach is “If I benefit from something (having them as my audience for the workshop to enroll them for my coaching), the others will feel being used.” Even though I believe that my workshop is valuable for them, my energy is probably not aligned with my intention since I have this belief that “people will feel being used.” I realized that this belief shows up multiple places in my life.

If I simply believe that my workshop is valuable and help people to set/approach their goals (without belief “they will feel being used”), I will be openly inviting people to my workshop without any hesitation. I actually enjoy talking about workshop and inviting them. My energy will be very different.

If I’m going to deliver the same content (goal setting), this workshop is for someone:

1) Someone who felt a surprising emptiness after reaching a goal. Someone who thought achieving it would bring joy, but the fulfillment didn’t last as they expected.

2) Someone who struggle to maintain results. They start over again the next year.

3) Someone who set goals but barely take action—or give up without making real progress.

4) Someone who can’t find purpose or passion in their life.

I understand what I need to believe in my head, but my body doesn’t follow with that belief…

How can I install this belief “my workshop/service is beneficial for them and they want to attend” in my body so that I can interact with people with better energy?

Thank you very much for your help.

**ANSWER:**

Yes, coach – It seems you’re singularly focused on the benefit you’d receive instead of the incredible value they’ll get in exchange for their time with you.

The whole point of business is to have an exchange of mutually beneficial things – that when exchanged, BOTH parties are satisfied (at minimum) with the transaction.

And when both parties benefit, everyone’s lives are improved.

And when done well, the client thinks they received a steal of a deal.

You asked, “
How can I install this belief “my workshop/service is beneficial for them and they want to attend” in my body so that I can interact with people with better energy?”

* To do this, you’ve got to be clear on the outcome of the workshop. What are the specific things (ideas, feelings, beliefs, etc.) they’ll leave your workshop with?

* How valuable are those things to your clients? (What is their life like without those things? What is different in their life when they have those things? Who else benefits by them having those things?)

You could do workshops once a month. That might be with new content or the same content. Or maybe you repeat the workshops once a quarter – lots of options. Having workshops around the same time each month could work really well for talking about them in meetups. They you are always talking about the next workshop or even the one after that.

What do you think might work best for you? Let’s make a plan for the first half of the year.

## Confidence in Creating Consults and Creating Income

**Date:** 2024-10-03  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

I’m working though building the belief that I absolutely CAN book consults, or create them.

From Ask A Coach yesterday

“Your mind is thinking that since you can’t control another human booking a consult, that you can’t really control if you book a consult or not. Same with making money. If you can’t make someone buy because you can’t control them, then you can’t control if you create money or not.

You also control who and how many people you meet. If we know that people want your offer, then we know it’s just a matter of meeting those people. Do people want your offer? What do you think?”

I’ve done some work reflecting on my attitude and confidence in how I show up. And trusting that that is enough for people to say yes to a consult. I also heard Stacey say in one of the modules today a way to describe a consult that makes a lot of sense for me.

A consult is a a time to get more clear on the scope of the problem, offer a prescription or pathway to the desired solution and see if the client and I are a fit to do that work together.

This helped me feel more ease about consults because I can now verbalize to myself that I don’t have to solve anyone’s big problems on the consult. that’s not the expectation. Creating clarity in the problem is a huge gift to my clients in itself.

It is true that I can control how many people I meet. That was a great reminder. So I’m taking action by making it a point to daily make personal connections online and in person. I also have some follow ups from networking events I can take action on.

What has come up for me is that I lack belief that I can create income from coaching- that its truly in my control.

My thought is that I can work hard, do all the right things, and its still outside of my control if someone buys or not.

I’d love some reframes or other thoughts to “try on” instead.

Thank you

**ANSWER:**

“My thought is that I can work hard, do all the right things, and its still outside of my control if someone buys or not.”

Let’s use an example for this.

Let’s say you have a friend who loves baking. She works full time right now, but she’d love to be a baker and sell cookies and cakes and pastries instead. She’s going to start in her home first and build up some business.

Do you think that she can make money?

Do you think she can make enough money to leave her full time job? Why or why not?

Bring this back so we can start to see your thoughts around having a business in general. Then we can look at your coaching business and see how it’s the same and how it’s different.

---

## AAC # 204 Developing the confidence that I can and have got the skill to Bridge The Gap

**Date:** 2024-07-09  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

Thank you. I think when I will believe that I have enough to bridge the gap will be when I can see their model and be like “oh, when they are thinking this, they are feeling this, and that’s causing them to do that. And that’s causing them to be in this situation. And I can see that maybe they don’t even realise that’s what’s happening.”

And when those results match up with their challenge or problem (reason they got on the consult) that is going to help them see more clearly how they are creating their current result. Which is a way forward to help them see how with coaching and awareness, I can help them shift into the feelings that would have them taking actions towards their goal that they do want.

And. I’ll still need to notice and let go of the thought “I can’t see it all. I don’t have enough to explain everything that’s happening.”

Making sure to have a clear idea of why they got on the consult call (what they want to work on) will help me match my bridging the gap with their goal.

It will make sense to bridge it effectively when I (a) know how their current actions are contributing to their current result, and the thought and feeling that is making them do that AND when I (b) have invited them to share their 10’s. What a 10 would look like in that particular area. And when I (c) also can even imagine and believe in advance for them a slightly more incredible 10. For the times when I CAN see that we can accomplish more in coaching than they can currently envision for themselves.

I don’t know what inspires me to doubt the gap I’ve bridged. Maybe when it just feels like I’ve talked too much, or repeated myself, or haven’t explained it clearly.

When I have done a really good bridging the gap, I’ve also had time to explain how coaching with me works, creating awareness, not telling them what to do, 12 weekly sessions etc. And I’m also able to speak very clearly, passionately but with certainty, about the vision and impact that coaching can create for them.

I don’t think I’ve ever really had the situation where they don’t see it as the solution they were imagining. It’s more just they don’t understand it as clearly as I would have liked to explain it.

I could pay closer attention to the client and check in more. But I don’t feel this is the sticking point here because I’m usually feeling fairly connected to them as a client and being able to serve them.

I’d love your help to keep creating awareness around this.

**ANSWER:**

It sounds like you’re connected to them, which is great. When you bridge the gap, THIS is the time to check in with them.

Do they see themselves following your prescription and inevitably creating their desired results? If yes, you have a solid bridge. If no, that’s where you’ll explore what’s preventing them from believing they can get the results they want from following your prescription (bridge.)

You shared, “
they don’t understand it as clearly as I would have liked to explain it.”
 What specifically isn’t clear for them? And why?

What specifically is their obstacle? Why is it there?

You shared you’re thinking, “
I can’t see it all. I don’t have enough to explain everything that’s happening.”

* There’s no need to see it all, or explain everything that’s happening. Only the things related to their problem and solving it. What do you think?

---

## A set back in my confidence

**Date:** 2024-07-09  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

Hi, I have a client (a couple – I couch both couples and individuals) who’s about to complete 6 month with me. They have been struggling and I often feel like Im not helping them as much as I promised, not even close. Incidentally one of them is a coach. All my other clients are experiencing transformation. My experience with these two is like smoke screens, I get lost in the weeds, they seem to be unwilling to be influenced by each other, or by me. When they experience some progress it follows by some terrible fight. This has affected my confidence. I see myself marketing less, doubting myself more. Thank god I have other clients that are thriving. But I see myself getting caught on these two. Im disappointed in myself and I sometimes dread our sessions. I keep coming back to curiosity, love, holding them in their greatness, trusting their power, evaluating after each session. And its definitely affect my making offers and marketing offers. I’d love your feedback and coaching on this?

**ANSWER:**

You shared with this couple: My experience with these two is like smoke screens, I get lost in the weeds, they seem to be unwilling to be influenced by each other, or by me.

What do you think is making you get lost in the weeds? Do you/the couple have clear goals or outcomes that you are looking for?

You share that they don’t seem to be willing to be influence by you or by each other. Have you brought this up with them in a gentle way? What are they both wanting? How do they need to show up in order to create that result?

We want to look at those questions, because we want you to get objective instead of letting this affect your confidence. Some clients will be more challenging than others. That’s okay. They can teach you the most. They might teach you who isn’t a fit for your style of coaching. They might teach you how to explain things in different ways. etc. What do you think this couple is here to teach you?

## Consult Confidence – Part 3

**Date:** 2024-04-16  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

Good to know!

I’d like to stick with the wheel until I feel like I’ve figured it out. I like the idea of doing a brief overview and then backtracking to the areas that are particularly challenging.

I think where I get stuck is I’m helping them troubleshoot a bit as far as ways to make it a 10. Otherwise it feels awkward to move on if we’re just leaving them hanging. But I realize that is just a thought…

It making me realize that I think I struggle with trying to provide value on the call. I want my calls to be a great use of their time and I’m not sure they are yet.

Any thoughts on that? Thank you!

**ANSWER:**

When you’re thinking of ways to help them make it a 10, do they not know what their goal is in that area? If not, it’s likely something they haven’t given much thought to.

You could even ask them to rate the importance of improving that area of their life. If might be that they’re willing to put their relationships on hold while they focus on their health, or vice versa. Just because it’s on the list doesn’t mean you have to give it equal time.

Let’s take a look at what’s happening on the call.

When you consider your consult, what do you currently view as valuable?

* How do you feel when you think about offering those things?

What are all the things your client sees as valuable from their time with you? (What you imagine they think – unless they’ve told you directly.)

* How do you feel when you think about offering those things?

## Consult Confidence – Part 2

**Date:** 2024-04-15  
**Topics:** confidence

**QUESTION:**

Very helpful response. I tend to get ahead of myself and this is a good reminder that I need way more information from the client before deciding what’s next.

I was listening to the consult module this morning and I think I may need to tweak my intake piece (where are they now) based on how I heard it this morning and on your prior response.

I use the wheel method and for the 4 consults I’ve done so far I’ve never gotten through more than 3 areas of life. Maybe I’m going too slow and asking too many questions that aren’t really leading to me gathering the right / or enough information.

Any guidance/coaching you can provide on that piece of the puzzle would be ver helpful!

Thank you!

**ANSWER:**

Absolutely, coach. If the intake is taking you more than 20 minutes, you’re likely getting off track somehow.

How do you feel when you think of tailoring the wheel to the areas which are most challenging for them? E.g. Briefly going over the wheel, and then focusing on the areas which are less than 5?

Other coaches opt for the conversational intake method shared in the “Where Are They” video under Consults, which allows the client to bring up the issues most pressing for them.

How do you feel when you think of doing that?

## Day 59 – follow up on day 58, my brain is wanting to choose imposter syndrome, confusion, victim mode

**Date:** 2025-12-22  
**Topics:** imposter

**QUESTION:**

Thank you for helping me reflect on these things!

I don’t think it’s true that I don’t know what’s valuable – because I know what’s valuable to ME. I think the thought is more accurately, “other people don’t find this valuable.” Which again is worth questioning. Because if I find something valuable, surely other people do too?

Things that are valuable to me when it comes to coaching:

-having someone hold belief for me when I don’t believe in myself (I don’t know that I offer this currently, I think my thought is that I’m not super confident in getting people tangible results and I also don’t even know what I offer currently, so it feels unaligned to say I believe they can achieve the thing no matter what)

-having someone to point out my thoughts so I can see how they are impacting me and question them (I do feel like I offer this now but also my imposter syndrome is saying I’m not doing it well, like I’m not the best at pointing out thoughts and when I do people don’t like it)

-having someone to ask powerful questions that help me be intentional and inspired (I think I do this ok, but I also know my imposter syndrome is weighing on my right now)

-seeing an example of someone who has gone through what I’m going through and knows exactly how to get me to the other side (I’m not where I want to be with this yet, but again, imposter syndrome and not giving myself credit)

“My audience doesn’t find anything I do valuable.” I’ve come to this conclusion because if they did, they would be paying me. It feels very true. But I can see an opportunity to better sell myself on my offer and why it’s amazing, as well as to marketing MORE in order to help other people see the value too.

That thought is similar to “they don’t want anything I try to offer.” Again, this feels true because nobody is reaching out. But again, I think if I work on better selling myself and selling them, it might help.

What do I help clients with? → trusting themselves, making plans and following through, setting boundaries, getting clear on what they want and giving themselves permission to have it

Who do I serve and how? → Women who want to take better care of themselves, show up for themselves, and feel happier and more lit up by their lives. How? Through coaching? I’m feeling a bit stuck on how to further explain that but I can also see it might be helpful…

What are my current offers? → 1:1 coaching for 6 months

The last 3 answers feel incomplete which is why I feel stuck I think.

**ANSWER:**

Hi Coach,

Thanks for taking the time to answer each of the questions. You did great getting specific!

Let’s dig into some of your answers and explore further.

You mentioned having “imposter syndrome” quite often in your answers.

First question is: 

 — Why do you feel like an imposter? If you had to list out each reason, what would they be?

You also shared a thought you have is:

“Other people don’t find this valuable.” 
And you said it feels true because,

“if they did find it valuable, they would be reaching out and paying you.”

You then shared why coaching is valuable 
to you
 – and I think it’s important we look at each of these.

1. Having someone 
hold belief for me
 when I don’t believe in myself.

2. Having someone to 
point out my thoughts
 so I can see how they are impacting me and question them.

3. Having someone to 
ask powerful questions
 that help me be intentional and inspired.”

4. 
Seeing an example
 of someone who has gone through what I’m going through and knows exactly how to get me to the other side.

These are such valuable reasons to have a coach in our corner. That leads me to you saying:

“I can see an opportunity to 
better sell myself on my offer
 and why it’s amazing, as well as 
marketing MORE
 in order to 
help other people see the value
 too.”

This stood out – because you listed above key things that are valuable when having a coach.

Is it possible in your marketing, you’re not sharing/conveying that enough?

How can you convey the value of coaching more?

For example, do you feel confident you could market what you shared on 1-4 as reasons a coach is valuable? Could you create examples for your audience where they feel like you get them, understand them, and know the problems they are facing and how you 
(as a coach)
 can help?

For example, you said one of the things you help clients with is setting boundaries.

– How can you market to the person who is terrible at setting boundaries, or doesn’t know how?

– What might that client be experiencing in their day-to-day that makes them need stronger boundaries?

– Can you, in your marketing, give examples so they realize that is them and then convey the power of having a coach to help them with that?

That’s just one example since you also said,
 “trusting themselves, making plans and following through, & getting clear on what they want and giving themselves permission to have it”

Do you feel confident you can hold belief for your clients, ask powerful questions, point out their thoughts
 (even if they don’t always like it),
 and simply be an example of doing this type of work?

You don’t necessarily have to have all the results you want yet, because you are still an example of showing up, getting coached, and getting value from it. What if that is what your potential clients need more of too? To consistently see examples of why/how coaching can be valuable?

Take some time to reflect on any of these questions that resonate and bring back your answers / what you’d like more coaching on!

## Imposter Syndrome -or- Building Belief?

**Date:** 2024-10-03  
**Topics:** imposter

**QUESTION:**

I’m a business coach. And, when I think about a business coach image I see someone who is suited/well dressed, bold, direct, matter-of-fact, and put together. Also, when I see someone dressed like that, I tend to think they’re successful and make a lot of money. I have never taken professional photos, but I would love to get some done in a cute black modern suit. Generally, when I dress more formally for special events, I feel powerful. It feels good. However, in the day to day, I’m the type that dresses more casually. But, I would say that even my casual look has some modern style to it. People have told me they like my “style”. When I think of getting photos done in a suit and using them for my marketing, some thoughts come up:

• When I dress like this for photos and not in the day to day, people will think I’m faking my success. (Discouraged)

• When people who know me see me dressed like this, they think I’m a fraud. (Embarrassed/Insecure)

• I can’t use photos like that because people will think I’m doing well and making a lot of money when I’m not. (Insecure/Ashamed)

• When my audience finds out I’m don’t have results like the image portrays, they’ll be disappointed and think I’m a fraud. (Ashamed/Embarrassed)

• I can’t take photos like that because it’s deceiving. (Discouraged/Sad)

• I haven’t earned the right to take those kinds of photos yet because I don’t have the results to back up the image. (Discouraged/Sad)

• I don’t want to confuse anyone or for my image to send the wrong message. (Worry)

• When I dress like that, people will think I’m unapproachable or out-of-reach. (Sad)

My main question is how much of this is me disqualifying myself because of what other people think and how much of this is part of the journey to building belief in who I see myself as? Maybe it’s both? I know there’s no right or wrong, but there’s still a part of me that also thinks it’s wrong for more to show up in my marketing this way.

Thank you, AAC.

**ANSWER:**

There are a few pieces to this. Let’s separate them out.

There’s an idea that you should look a certain way as a business coach. Do you think that people wouldn’t hire you if you are dressed in your casual clothes? Let’s break down this idea a bit. There is an old school idea of how you should dress for work. But what about Steve Jobs? Or Mark Zuckerberg. Or Ellen. You can also look at some of Stacey’s past videos. If you go under the Bonus section, there is a link to the Interrupt Training. Look at how Stacey is showing up then vs. now.

Then there are all of these thoughts about what other people will think about you.

Let’s think about just your clients for a second and not worry about the other people. You might imagine a client or think about one of your favourite past clients. What do you want to be wearing when you are with them in a session? What helps you show up as the best coach you can be?

I’ll give you an example of how I think about it.

So one on one, they usually just see the top of me, so often I’ll have a blanket on my legs. It’s comfy and I’m relaxed. I don’t want to be worried about cleavage or anything like that – it distracts my own mind. It doesn’t feel as good for ME. So I wear things that have a bit more coverage. I also like to think I look “decent” (you get to define what that is for you). Again, because if I’m thinking about my hair or what I look like I’m not focused on the client.

Then we have the part where you are so focused on what a business coach should look like and what other people think, that it’s blocking you from seeing/doing what really works for you. If we didn’t have this image of what you should look like to be a business coach and we weren’t worried that all these people would judge us, then you’d be free to explore. Maybe you want some photos in a suit. Maybe you want jeans and a nice top. Maybe you show up in your stories on IG straight after the gym in workout clothes one day, but in a dress other days. Maybe you like to put on some makeup before you show up live on IG or maybe you don’t. This is going to help you show up in a way that makes sense for you and who you are. Maybe you don’t want to show up in stained, torn clothes OR maybe you do. Maybe you are a creative person and when you paint you are in your paint clothes and you want to share that sometimes. WHO do you want to show up as with your clients?
