Getting harder and harder to coach myself in my biz. More fear and sadness about biz and life than ever. I’ll always coach (do so as an employee and professional); BUT selling inspires me/my passion! I love it but not sure if I want it for financial freedom anymore. I don’t want selling coaching to be a “side gig” I don’t subscribe to that mentality.

Please help me understand the circumstances. Things I can control and cannot.

I fear reaching retirement age not with a biz that supports my basic life. Yes, have a fully funded IRA, now selling coaching is more than about the money. Failure about the fear of a waisted life; getting to 101y and die as a failure without making a biz of my dreams: one that supports my basic needs, don’t need much money.

Makes me want to stop focusing on revenue or inviting to consults. I love money and I love selling, but maybe they don’t go together. The fear of failure is ruining the passion.

**ANSWER:**

We have a bit of a discrepancy happening right now.

In the last 12 months you made $500.

You love selling. But you aren’t selling.

We want to get curious about why. Not just by doing evaluations because you have done those – but really deeply curious here and open. What is keeping you from selling? Why aren’t people buying?

It might be that you DO love selling – but you don’t like working for yourself. You like a team or a group environment and by yourself isn’t where you thrive.

It might be that you like the IDEA of selling – but you don’t actually want to put in the work. Again – no judgement. That doesn’t mean you aren’t SUPER hard working – it might be that this just isn’t your thing.

There might be something else – like some other priority or stress is factoring in.  etc.

When you really dig deep – what do you think it is? What is the part of business/selling that you are struggling with?

## Wanting to move forward but fear is holding me back Part 3

**Date:** 2025-07-21  
**Topics:** fear

**QUESTION:**

So I went in, did my pitch, and the manager had no expression on her face (it was like she was waiting for me to get to the point). I tried to stay confident and certain despite her expression lol. And gave her my brochure with what I offer and how my coaching enhances her medispa treatments.

She said she never really thought about adding weight loss and wellness coaching to her medispa clinic. She said she would look it over and think about it and get back to me. I also gave her my business card. She asked where my business was and I told her online. I told her it would be virtual so there would be no overhead on her part. I explained it is a referral partnership. I took her’s business card as well. And I’m planning to follow up next week.

What went well:

I went in with confidence

Said my pitch well

Gave her the information (brochure and card)

Answered her questions

Planning on following up

What didn’t go well:

I went out of my brain for a minute when I saw her expression and got nervous (Thought: I’m bothering her).

When she asked where’s by business I could have also said that if she would like me to do it in person I could also do that a few times a week, whatever works for her.

What could I have done better:

Believe more in myself, my product and offer so I have unshakeable faith regardless of anyone else’s reaction

Stop seeking validation from outside sources and instead look for it internally (I want a yes but don’t need it to succeed, I will anyways because it’s in my hands)

Practice possible Q/A’s that may come up

I have a new thought after that: there are not enough businesses for me to go to that would accept my offer.

C: Medispa Partnership

T: There are not enough medispa businesses for me to go to that would accept my offer.

F: scarce

A: Continue to go to medispas but with feeling of a little bit desperation

R: Not enough medispa’s willing to accept my offer

**ANSWER:**

You showed up and you shared your offer! That’s huge. Doing that is how you are going to get confident. It’s how it will one day feel totally natural to tell someone about what you do and pitch them.

You are thinking there might not be enough medi spas in order for you to be able to have a successful business by partnering with them. You don’t know that though. It could be that you partner with 1 of them and that is enough to fill your business. It could also be that you partner with a few and that’s part of where your clients come from. This is just one possible way for you to sign clients. It has you showing up and talking about what you do.

What are your next steps for this week? Is it going to the other medi spas, following up with this person, etc?

## Fear of Making Money Day 5

**Date:** 2025-06-16  
**Topics:** fear

**QUESTION:**

Y’all’s insights and prompts have been SO helpful. We’ve been talking about my block to making $1,500 steady per month.

I am working with a money coach, and we’ve been unpacking SO many money fears and stealth beliefs around money. Now that a lot of the shame has been removed I can see that I also have a belief that it’s not safe for me to make money, That I can’t be trusted with money. That I will inadvertently blow any money I make.

We have about at $1,000 per month deficit in our household budget, hence why I have a goal of making $1,500/mo coaching.

My money coach has suggested I get a part time job. in order to take the pressure off as my coaching offer builds. Something very part time like UberEats or Rover, dog sitting.

While thinking about watching dogs at my house I had the thought that I would need to get more dog crates (to be able to contain the extra dogs when they are not playing).

This single thought had me careening into a thought that getting a job is pointless because I will spend money while trying to make money. Cancelling out any money made for the family.

(And there are facts behind this story- I have had previous product based, farming businesses, and any profits I made had to be reinvested in the business because that was “the only” way to produce more for the next year.

And I realize that I feel powerless to this- like I automatically make poor choices with money, and it happens without my consent. Which of course isn’t true. And I bet its part of the unconscious beliefs that are making it hard to make money/charge what I would like to, etc.

I am part way there already- realizing this pattern, but what questions can I journal about to further explore this? OR do I just need to work on an intentional model around this?

**ANSWER:**

In your past business, you would need to spend a lot on inputs in order to make money again the next year.

And you weren’t making enough each year to have the profit you want and enough set aside for these inputs too.

I am hearing from you that you have learned from that and want to figure out how to make more profit.

You are already thinking about it with the dog crates. You want to make sure that you can recover the money you spent on the crates AND bring in income. Can you do that? How much are the crates and how many months would it take to recover that cost? Once you have the crates – then most of the rest of what you will make will be profit. (after taxes/other small expenses)

People aren’t born just knowing what to do with money. It’s something you learn and can get support in doing. You won’t know about business expenses and taxes and costs and profit margins and investments and interest rates when you come out of the womb as a baby. No one knows those things. It’s something people are taught – and some of us aren’t taught it at all or are told things that keep us struggling with money. So – don’t expect that you should know these things. It’s something you can learn.

And you ARE learning. You are looking at your budget – you’d like $1000 to cover it and an extra $500 in there too. You know where that money would go – it would go to breaking even in your budget. And then where would you put the rest of the money? Maybe it’s savings for taxes right not or business expenses so you can re-invest in your business when you want to. Let’s decide that now so you can drop this story that you’d blow the money.

## Fear about meeting people AAC 6/30

**Date:** 2025-04-30  
**Topics:** fear

**QUESTION:**

This morning, I was filling out the Organic Marketing worksheet, and I realized I have a massive fear of meeting people. I will list several thoughts and experiences that I have around that, and my making offers before, that I think are massively influencing the way I show up now.

Experiences (I would put these under C because they are true and have happened) – When I tell people I’m a coach, their reactions are: What is that, Everybody’s a life coach these days, or I don’t need a coach. To put it in short, there’s A LOT of misunderstanding and prejudice around what coaching is, here in Serbia. It happened a few times (online) where I really saw that I could help someone, and I said that, and the reaction was: oh no, I don’t need that. It really gets to me how people here are prejudiced towards coaching, it’s almost looked down upon.

Meeting people: I am by nature very curious, and I am the one always asking people questions. It happens too often that it never comes to me talking about myself and what I do. Or, if I say I’m a coach, I get reactions like above. I have NEVER encountered someone who wanted to know more about that, or how I can help them.

My thoughts (which are hard for me to object): People here will ridicule me for offering coaching; they don’t need or want coaching

All in all, these experiences have really impacted me, and I always feel like I need to justify coaching or overexplain and it sounds like I’m wanting to convince someone. I’ve even had situations with clients who, as we get closer to the end of the program, subconsciously undermine the process of coaching. One person even called it brainstorming, the other person after 3 months (and I helped her immensely) said she ”got nothing concrete” and that she got more out of one Family Constellations workshop…

I’m sorry for writing such a long question, but these things and my environment really affect me. I’ve been coaching for 2 years, and I feel like I’m swimming against the currents.

**ANSWER:**

Let’s shift how you are thinking about meeting people.

When you are meeting people – you want to try to be meeting people who are looking for what you offer. For example, if you are a parenting coach, you want to be meeting people who are looking for help with parenting. You want to put yourself in places and get in front of those people. We want to be thinking about and focused on the people who DO want help.

Thinking about your offer, what kind of people are you wanting to meet? Where could you meet people who ARE looking for what you offer? Your mind might feel stuck answering this, but we need to keep pushing past that and starting to answer this question.

For example, if you do general life coaching – maybe you help women step into their true selves – then you want to be in places where those women will be. Maybe it’s being a guest on a podcast that talks about personal development, maybe it’s being in Facebook groups that talk about personal development so you can better understand how your clients talk about their problems.

Those people want coaching. Those are the people you want to be thinking about and meeting.

Tell me about those people. How are you going to start spending more time interacting with and meeting those people who do want your offer?

Watch the video in the Organic Marketing module called Energy of 2k Today. There will be lots of people who are a no. You want to focus on the ones who want to be a yes.

You aren’t wanting to record from thinking you are great – you are wanting to record to learn and to  be able to get your message out to more people. ## Rid of the uncertainty

**Date:** 2019-10-23  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I would like coaching on how to decide when to stop and really work my beliefs and when it is time to just take action regardless of the emotion I am feeling. There are days when I experience one negative thought after another (especially now that I am pregnant and experiencing near constant morning sickness and fatigue) and I want to know if/when it is just best to do all the good things I know to do, even when my energy is bad.

My ideal outcome would be to get rid of the uncertainty I keep creating about “do I need to change my beliefs or just act – because aren’t all my actions taken from negative energy going to give me negative results anyway?”

**ANSWER:**

There comes a point where self-work can turn into self-sabotage.  When your thought work keeps you from meeting new people, telling them you’re a life coach and making offers to help them, it’s a hinderance, not a help.

So, yes.  Just. Take. Action.

Are you waiting to feel “ready”?  You won’t at first.  Take action anyway.

Even when you feel bad.

Even when you’re tired.

Make it non-negotiable.

Make showing up non-negotiable.

Make meeting new people non-negotiable.

Make making offers non-negotiable.

Make making money non-negotiable.

Don’t try and be something that you aren’t, and make sure whatever you do resonates with you.  But do something.  Then keep on doing.  You can do that even when you are feeling bad, tired, low, etc.

It’s like taking care of that baby. You still do it, even when you feel bad. It’s non-negotiable.

It may not be 100% . . . but we can do a lot with 20%.  And without question, 20% gets you a lot further to achieving results than 0%.

## Deadlines impacting beliefs

**Date:** 2019-10-23  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I have worked to allow my beliefs to be the #1 priority in my biz. My business and I have grown together so much over the last 3 months! Making more than I ever have in my biz. I did a lot of work using the 2K method and my 1:1 coach to drop the hustle, lean into my beliefs, and trust that they’d work for me. THEY TOTALLY HAVE, so fun! But, one thing I have NOT been able to do, is achieve a goal within a certain time frame. When I add any sort of deadline, all the stuff comes up. I avoid, I create new goals in stead… I tell myself that that goal doesn’t sound exciting, so I change it… TIME has power over my ACTIONS and results, I’d love some help on this. I do have a specific example — I set a goal to have 30K by September 24th (my 30th bday). I’ve totally avoided, because I make drama when I think about the time that’s left and deadlines. It impacts my belief.

To still lean into amazing beliefs and have that calm, peaceful, confident, sureness in my business, but also learn how to stay in that belief AS I am also working within a time frame. Not letting that freak me out. LOLLLsss…

**ANSWER:**

First off…congrats on your progress.  I love the way that you say ” My business and I have grown together so much over the last 3 months!” Right this minute I’m picturing you and your biz as this cute little couple who have been through it and are still totally delighted by each other’s company.  (In this vision, you and your biz are on a park bench. There are ice cream cones and a bouquet of flowers. You and your biz are holding hands. All the fixings really. Totally adorable.)

With that said, let’s get down to brass tacks with what’s not adorable–your issue with deadlines.

Time doesn’t have control over your actions.  Time is neutral.  So are deadlines.  Time is a Circumstance.  Deadlines are too.

Put your goal/deadline in the C line.  Complete two models: an unintentional one and an intentional one.

Unintentional Model

C: $30k by Sept. 24th

T:

F:

A: Avoid doing work. Tell yourself original goal doesn’t sound exciting. Abandon goal.  Create new goals instead.  Waste time. Buffer. Indulge in overwhelm. Don’t take action.

R: Don’t make $30k by Sept. 24th

How do you feel when you think about that C?  You didn’t hit your deadline because you avoided it and the work you needed to do to reach it. Notice though that you’re not just avoiding the work; you’re avoiding whatever emotion is in your F line too.

What do you have to be willing to feel in order to not avoid?

Intentional Model

C: $30k by Sept. 24th

T:

F:

A: Show up. Offer value. Serve. Meet people. Tell them you’re a coach. Make offers. Inspire. Book consults. Sign clients.

R: Make $30k by Sept. 24th

What would you need to think and feel about your goal in order to take action despite your discomfort?

## Identifying feelings

**Date:** 2019-10-23  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I am struggling to identify which feelings I am having – I seem to be able to identify anxiety or worry but nothing else. Do you have tips for identifying other less obvious feelings?

I would like to be able to identify a broader spectrum of feelings and know them by the sensations they give me in my body. I think I’ve got very “stuck” on anxiety because it’s been the predominant one I’ve felt over the last few years. I think it’s dulled my emotional senses.

**ANSWER:**

Get yourself a list of emotions. Go on “the Google” and search “List of Emotions.” There’s your list.

Feeling anxious has not dulled your emotional senses. Emotions are just vibrations in our bodies. Our emotions don’t have the ability to do ANYTHING other than creating vibrations in our bodies.

If you want to uncover what you’re feeling, if you want a better answer, you need to ask better questions.

Allowing emotions is a skill.  It takes practice.  Instead of waiting to do it with negative emotions only, you can practice feeling and identifying emotions with positive emotions too.

Pay attention all day long. Set an alarm on your phone. Have it go off every hour on the hour. Every time it goes off, check in with yourself.  Ask yourself what F you’re feeling in that exact moment.

If you can’t name it immediately, look at your list of emotions. Run through it. What jumps out at you? What feels right?

Think about how you’re feeling? Even if you can’t name it, describe it. In detail. Where is it in your body. Describe the experience of feeling it in detail as if you’re describing it to a martian, someone who doesn’t even know what feelings are. Where are you feeling the vibration in your body? What color is it? Write it all down.

Then work backwards. What thought are you thinking that’s making you feel this way? What circumstance are you thinking about when you’re thinking those thoughts?

Just be a curious observer. Take notes. Use your list. Find the one that fits. Think of the emotions list as a thesaurus. You could use the word: mad. Or you could use the following:

Agitated

Aggravated

Amazed

Annoyed

Animosity

Appalled

Astonished

Betrayed

Bitter

Bothered

Challenged

Contemptuous

Cranky

Cross

Crummy

Detestation

Disdain

Disgruntled

Disgusted

Dumbfounded

Expectant

Floored

Frustrated

Hostile

Humiliated

Incensed

Indignant

Irritated

Miffed

Offended

Peevish

Perturbed

Proud

Repugnant

Scornful

Shaken

Sick

Sour

Stunned

Sullen

Surprised

Traumatized

Upset

Vindictive

If you don’t know the difference, use “the Google.” A dictionary is your best friend. Approach feelings with curiosity. All of the emotions listed above are more specific ways to describe “mad.” If one sounds like it fits, look it up. If the definition is spot on, then that’s what you’re feeling. If you compare it against a few others and another one feels like a better fit, then that’s the one. It’s not about perfection, but there is a certain value that comes from broadening your emotional vocabulary. Try this exercise with fun emotions like happy, excited, brave and aroused. When you feel those sorts of feelings, look at your list and try to find a more specific emotion. Study the list. Study yourself. Be as curious as George.

---

## Avoiding work

**Date:** 2019-10-23  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I know I want a business but often I don’t feel like working on my business. I see myself doing all the things to avoid working on my business – Netflix, socializing, telling myself I need more self care. I feel worse after doing these things, but often can’t get over the hump. I see it in my results – some clients here and there but I know I need to work more to bring in more clients.

I want to wake up looking forward to working on my business everyday. I want to know exactly what I should be doing each day. I want to be making $3k a month and consistently have clients.

**ANSWER:**

The first thing you said was “I know I want a business.” Is that true?

If you’re using the 5-Step Close method taught in 2k for 2k then you know that during a consult, you reach a point where you ask your potential clients this: “On a scale of 1-10, how bad do you want the result?”  Answer that for yourself.  Do a consult on yourself.  Lay out where you are and where you want to be.  Then bridge the gap.  Then paint the vision.  Then ask yourself on a scale of 1-10, how committed are you to getting the result you want, no matter the money, time, or what anyone else thinks about it or what you have to do to achieve it? Be honest with yourself. What’s that number? What are your objections that are preventing you from getting it? What would make that number a 10?

With that said, let’s look at your Ts about your business. Using the Model, you can see how your Ts are giving you your current Rs.

C: Business

T: I don’t feel like it.

F:

A: Buffer with Netflix, “self care,” socializing. Avoid doing actual work.

R: Feel bad about not working on business. Still don’t feel like it.

C: Business

T: I want a business.

F:

A: Buffer with Netflix, “self care,” socializing. Avoid doing actual work.

R: Still want/don’t actually have a business.

C: Business

T: I know I need to work more to bring in more clients.

F:

A: Buffer with Netflix, “self care,” socializing. Avoid doing actual work.

R: Don’t work more. Don’t bring in more clients.

In this last model, do you see how knowing what you need to do isn’t enough?  Think of it this way: so many people want to lose weight.  The solution is simple: eat fewer calories than you burn.  That’s it.  People know this.  Knowing isn’t enough.  Same goes for you with your business.  You know you need to work more, but you aren’t.  Why not?  What are you avoiding?  Find your Fs for the models up above and you’ll find your answer.

---

## Sticking with it

**Date:** 2019-10-23  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I’ve been in the 2K process for almost a month and I love it. However I’m struggling with showing up consistently. I feel like I’m all over the place. Do I even want to coach? Do I want to blog? Both? And when I show up I struggle to stay with a clear message. My niche are ambitious women who struggle with perfectionism but I also want to talk about other things. I really struggle to create new beliefs. I know what I need to do intellectually (showing up, providing value, clean selling) but I find it hard to stay focused and not wonder off and suddenly create lifestyle content. I created a FB group and when people didn’t engage in the group I just left it. I’m one of the people who try something enthusiastically once and when I don’t get an instant result I drop it. I know my lack of focus is self-sabotage but I find it hard to break out of that.

I want to be able to take action in a clear focused way. No wondering off. I want to be like: This is what I’m doing no matter what. I want to follow through on my plans. It’s funny how I coach perfectionists but I’m also going through the same things, because I’m a perfectionist. It’s like there is another person inside me who is taking over.

**ANSWER:**

C: Coaching business.

T: I’m all over the place.

F: Scattered.

A: Inaction. Judge yourself. Wallow in self-pity. Hide. Wait to take action until you “get your shit together.”

R: You’re no place.

That’s the model that comes from your T “I’m all over the place.”  Thinking it doesn’t get you the results you want.  Exercising constraint, being decisive and taking deliberate, consistent action likely will.

Run an intentional model.  Put the result you want in your R line. Then work your way up.  Figure out the steps you need to take to get that result and how you’ll have to think and feel in order to take them.

## Email from psychologist

**Date:** 2019-10-23  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I received an email from a psychologist who said my coaching practice was unethical – she accused me of practicing psychotherapy without a license. I know I am not, but feel unworthiness and fear coming up.

My ideal outcome would be gaining confidence, so that in the future I don’t spiral when I receive messages like these.

**ANSWER:**

What were the exact words she used? Put those in your C line.  I’m not sure if the description you gave is verbatim, so I’ll use the words you wrote.  (However, if she said something different, don’t put your interpretation of it in the C line, use the actual words she used in her email.)

Here are your models.  Figure out what your Ts are about what the psychologist said.

C: “Your coaching practice is unethical.”

T:

F: Afraid.

A: Spin out about what psychologist said./Take less action is business. Believe psychologist.

R:

C: “Your coaching practice is unethical.”

T:

F: Unworthy.

A: Spin out about what psychologist said./Take less action is business. Believe psychologist.

R:

That C is totally, 100 percent neutral.  You’re the one giving it meaning with your thoughts.

C: “You’re practicing psychotherapy without a license.”

T:

F: Afraid.

A: Spin out about what psychologist said./Take less action is business. Believe psychologist.

R:

C: “You’re practicing psychotherapy without a license.”

T:

F: Unworthy.

A: Spin out about what psychologist said./Take less action is business. Believe psychologist.

R:

Bottom line, you’re finding some truth in what the psychologist said.  If you weren’t you wouldn’t be bothered by her having said it.  This is not to say that there is truth in what the psychologist said.  You’re just believing there is.  You don’t have to.  You can belief something different.  You get to choose.

You can choose to think the same thing about the psychologist. You can choose to think she’s confused or that she just doesn’t understand coaching.

You can choose to make what she said mean something about you. You can also choose not to.  The choice is simply yours.

Find out what you’ve been choosing, and then decide what you want to choose going forward.  If those Ts are different, which they likely are, practice the latter and you’ll eventually believe them.

## Managing my mind

**Date:** 2019-10-23  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I want to be coached on how to manage my mind so I can coach strong, when I’m feeling negative emotions (aggravated, annoyed, angry) from private life drama. When I think “Clients are coming to me” I feel moved, and my action is to allow all emotions. But the feeling of being “moved” is slight and quickly passing. The action of allowing feels forced (maybe I’m doing it wrong?) I still feel mostly annoyed, angry, aggravated, because of a fight with my life partner. I want to learn how to power through negative emotions and whether there’s a way to not let one unintentional model from private life impact the intentional models I want to believe for my business life.

My ideal outcome would be for me to learn and apply what it really means to allow both negative and positive emotions so that I don’t buffer or self-sabotage. Additionally, I would master my own feelings so that I can be the product of my product and coach my clients to do the same. I believe that achieving emotional mastery leads to negotiation mastery. I want to be the example of what’s possible.

**ANSWER:**

Tell me how you’re spending the hour before a consult or a coaching call with a client?

If you set time aside to coach yourself, you’ll be focused on thoughts that serve you and your client for the best coaching session possible, instead of coming to a coach call or a consult on the “hot mess express” fresh off an argument with your SO.

Maybe you’re fighting with your partner up until five seconds before the coaching call is scheduled to begin.  Maybe you’re not.  Maybe the argument happened that morning, hours earlier, or the day before.  Whatever the case may be, it doesn’t matter.  You’re choosing to let it linger and you’re being indulgent in that choice.

You want to manage your mind so you show up on a client call able to coach from a clean place? You gotta coach yourself, sister. No ifs, ands, or buts about it.

C: Coaching session.

T: I can’t focus on my client’s issues because I’m so angry with / distracted by my partner.

F: Irate.

A: Don’t listen hard. Don’t coach hard. Show up completely distracted. Don’t show up as your best self.

R: Angry with yourself for showing up that way. Went through session distracted.

If you focus on serving your client, you won’t be able to focus on your negative thoughts regarding your partner.  You have to be disciplined enough though to set aside time to get into the state of mind your client deserves to have you be in on the call.  Make it mandatory for yourself.  No excuses.  Ask and answer these questions before a session: Who does your client need you to be during the session? Who do you want to be during the session?  How do you want to show up?  Are you committed to your client’s transformation?  How will you show up if you are?

Also, take a look at your T “I want to be the example of what’s possible.”  Run a model on that.  Now run a model on this slightly different T “I am an example of what’s possible.”  Do those two Ts create different feelings? What are they? What about actions? Any difference? Post both models in the FB community along with your thoughts about them.

A

## Essentialism

**Date:** 2019-10-23  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I’m doing a weekly FB live, meeting lots of people at classes/events, and I started making offers. Beyond that, I’m wondering what is absolutely essential for me to be doing daily as a biz building practice? I’m asking myself WHO do I need to become to be the coach that makes $ and that is also equating to what else do I need to DO?

I want to build my business! I’m ready to move from my current $0 to my first $2K.

**ANSWER:**

Your question “WHO do I need to become to be the coach that makes $?” implies that you – your current self – isn’t able to and reinforces this idea that you have to change in order to do so.  You don’t.  You have everything you need to make money as a coach.  No transformation necessary.  Watch the Making Money with the Model Video on Circumstances.  In the beginning of the video, there’s a whole discussion on how this is true.  You have everything you need, you just have to take action.

That being said, maybe hang up that question for a bit and try asking a different, more direct, one instead.  So rather than asking “WHO do I need to become to be the coach that makes money?” try “What do I need to think right now to get out there and connect with someone?”  Answering that question will give you action items, which ties into the second part of your question “what else do I need to DO?”

Try answering that for yourself.  Brainstorm.  Make a list.  Take action. Evaluate. Take more action. Coach. Rinse and repeat. If you find yourself feeling stuck turn to the Foundational Videos on the 2k for 2k Portal under the “Start Here” tab.  And, if all else fails, go back to the basics.  (1) Meet people.  (2) Tell them you’re a life coach.  (3) Make offers to help them.  Three steps.  That’s it.  It’s that simple.

Current Model

F: Nervous.

R: Get 3 free clients. Engagement on social media content.

Intentional Model

C: Coaching business.

T:

F:

A:

R: 3 paid clients./Year-end goal.

## Fleeting thoughts

**Date:** 2019-10-25  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

My feelings are fleeting— as a result of my fleeting thoughts. I find myself with tons of thoughts which shift my feelings. Although they tend to be positive in nature they aren’t enough to create massive action on most days. But when they do. It’s a wonderful productive day. I desire to be consistent and disciplined in this work and model.

My ideal outcome is that I am consistent and discipline with creating the action and results I want. I want to be committed EVERYTHING not just when I “feel” like it.

**ANSWER:**

Recognize how you’re giving your power away by how your thinking about thoughts.  While you’re recognizing that your thoughts create your feelings, you’re still viewing your thoughts as things that are outside of your control.  Also, you’re also not attributing your thoughts to a particular C.  Don’t skip the C.  There’s always a C, and the C is the basis of every model you run.

C: Thoughts.

T: My thoughts are fleeting.

F:

A:

R:

How does that thought make you feel?  When you feel that way, how do you show up/not show up in your business?

It sounds like you’re giving a negative meaning to the T “My thoughts are fleeting.”  Notice how that’s optional.  You get to decide what you make it mean.  What if that wasn’t a negative thing?  Each person thinks thousands and thousands of thoughts each day.  Couldn’t you say all thoughts are fleeting?  If your thoughts are fleeting, wouldn’t that means your negative thoughts are fleeting too?

Go to the 2k for 2k Portal, find the Facebook Lives Tab and watch the Oct. 21 Q & A Video.

Building belief is like building a muscle.  You have to do reps.  That’s how you build it.  How much time are you spending on practicing positive thoughts?  Are you expecting immediate results?  It’s like working out once or twice and expecting to be in great shape.  Doesn’t happen.  You’ve got to practice thinking new thoughts every single day…2k times a day.

Answer these questions: What do you want your clients to think about you as a coach?  What do you need to believe about you to show up as that coach?  Practice thinking those Ts. Practice thinking them so often that you don’t make as much space for the negative Ts to creep in.

I’ve been diagnosed with PTSD and I’m doing a lot of trauma healing with a therapist and work on my own. I’m having a hard time showing up as a coach when I feel so shattered in my own life. There are lots of places where I’m still functional and can hold space, but I’m also raw and exhausted and am afraid I can’t help anyone right now. I’m also struggling to share my story, since I’m so focused on the parts of me that aren’t healed and aren’t working in my life right now, rather than all the places I’ve healed myself and coached myself.

I’d like to know that I can be healing myself from my trauma while also coaching others. I’d like to not see everyone else as traumatized just because I am, and focus instead on things in them that I can help with. I’d like to see that just because my process has been slow – and that I feel stuck in my goals because of this work that needs to be done first – doesn’t mean I can’t hold someone else to their goals, or be an effective coach in getting them their results. Ultimately, I’d like to still show up as coach and make sales this year, even as I continue to heal myself.

**ANSWER:**

You don’t need to share things that still feel raw or that you aren’t comfortable sharing, but what would happen if you decided to believe the things that you say you want to believe right now? You could just believe them now. Try on those different beliefs that you want to believe and see who you would be if you believed them. If you like who you are when you believe those thoughts, then you can just believe them. It really is that simple.

Practice them everyday. Model out those beliefs that you want to believe.

C Trauma

T

F

A

R Have trauma and coach

The inability to function or coach when in trauma comes from the resistance to the feelings that you have when thinking about the trauma. What’s that feeling? It’s not from the trauma, but from the thoughts and feelings you have when you think about it. Knowing that isn’t about trying to fix it or make those feelings go away, it’s about letting go of the resistance around feeling those feelings. Work on feeling the feelings that come up for you.

## Seeking validation

**Date:** 2019-10-25  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

Struggling with believing HARD. Working through models makes me feel better in the immediate moment…but I am having a hard time carrying that throughout my day; feeling like a failure and not serving/signing clients.

I want to manage my feelings to be my best NO MATTER WHAT. My brain is seeking validation that I’m NOT worthy of success…and finding it.

**ANSWER:**

It sounds like when you’re doing models, you are able to slide into the second stage of belief– possibility.  Then, the rest of the day, you brain slowly starts to slip back into the first stage of belief–impossibility.  And when that happens, you think something’s gone wrong.  What if that wasn’t the case?  What if it was totally normal?

Brains like to think comfortable thoughts that they’re used to thinking.  New thoughts mean change is on the horizon.  Change is scary, so our brains say.

Instead of just practicing your new thoughts when you’re doing models, practice them all day.  Say them 2k times to yourself.

With that said, what if in addition to practicing those thoughts, you just allowed yourself to feel the negative feelings that you experience when you’re not “believing hard” and still took action anyway?  What do you need to believe about you and the people you want to serve in order to embrace the discomfort and just forge ahead?

## Too salesy

**Date:** 2019-10-26  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I’m a brand new coach and just launching my business this week. I want to start getting out there meeting people, telling them I’m a life coach, and making offers to help them. My problem is that I can shake the thought that I’m going to come across salesy.

I want to go out into the world in true belief that clients will see the value in coaching and gravitate to me.

**ANSWER:**

Yay for launching your business! Let’s look at the model you have going on right now. How do you feel and what to you do when you think “I’m going to come across as salesy”?

C business

T I’m going to come across salesy.

F

A

R

There is a video in the 2k process, under consults, called How Not To Be Salesy. You will find it super useful. I’ll give you the short version here.

We feel salesy when we are thinking that we have to make a sale. If we are in our own thoughts and worried about not finding a client or feeling pressure to hit a goal, then we push for the sale and come across as salesy. The trick to not being salesy is to always be in service. If you are focused on what’s best for your client, and not on your own agenda, then you can offer to help in a way that feels good instead of salesy. Salesy doesn’t come from the actions you are doing, but from the thoughts and feelings you have while doing them. What would you have to think to get out there and meet people without feeling salesy?

C business

T

F

A I meet people. I tell them I’m a life coach. I make offers to help.

R

What would you have to think to behave in a professional manner at your full time job and as a life coach?

C business & full time job

T

F

I start, I stop. I indulge in confusion. I ask myself, “what makes an offer“. I make a plan, I don’t follow it. I ask myself what should I be doing today?-The answer is I don’t know. I think I’m doing my work, but I can see that I don’t have any results.
My ideal outcome would be to create a plan, even one step into the future, and follow it and stick with it through doubt and confusion until I create consults and clients.

When I think this T, and feel this F, and do these actions then I get this result.

What could you think to help you follow the 2k process and help yourself stay out of confusion?

C

T

F

A I create a plan and I follow the plan.

R

## Struggling to change my thoughts

**Date:** 2019-10-26  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I am a victim to my circumstances. I see this in every approach that i make to build my business. I blame my husband for not supporting my dreams. I believe that building my business should look a certain way. I think “having it all” means that others are on board with what i believe and how I want to build my business. I have coached myself a lot around these beliefs and know they aren’t true. Learning to let them go is another story:)

I want to work from home (can’t because my home isn’t mine and my husband won’t watch the kids all the way when I’m there). I want my results to be that I’m making 6k a month. I want my results to be a full time coaching practice of 20 clients. I have 6

**ANSWER:**

Here is what you think is happening:

C husband doesn’t watch the kids all the way when I am at home

R I can’t work on  my business.

Here is what is really happening.

C husband doesn’t watch the kids all the way when I am home

T I can’t build a business if he isn’t watching the kids.

F frustrated

A I don’t work on my business. I blame my husband for not supporting me.

R I don’t make 6k a month.

We feel like a victim to our circumstances when we don’t realize that we are creating our own results with our own thoughts. It can totally be true that your husband doesn’t watch the kids when you are at home, but what you think about that is going to determine how you feel, what you do, and what results you get. C husband doesn’t watch the kids all the way when I am home

T

F

A

R $6k/month

## Consistency

**Date:** 2019-10-26  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I want to earn $5k in my biz this month (and continue to earn that and more throughout the rest of the year) and am working on believing that I actually can do it. But when it comes to taking action and staying consistent, I fail. The thoughts I’m intentionally thinking feel great, but my actions aren’t aligning. What am I doing wrong? Giving up too fast because I’m not getting instant gratification? LOL.

I want to create $5k of biz income this month and I want my actions to be consistent

**ANSWER:**

If the thoughts you are thinking feel great, but don’t produce the actions that you need to reach your goal, then those thoughts aren’t the right thoughts to think if you want to reach your goal.

I’ll show you an example of a model that would feel good but that wouldn’t produce results.

C Goal: $5k this month

T Clients are coming so I’ll wait here for them.

F happy

A I wait for clients to come to me. I don’t make offers. I don’t work the 2k process.

R Clients don’t come this month. I don’t make $5k.

Do you see how the thought seems like a good thought but isn’t the right thought for that person? A thought can feel good and not be the right thought to compel us to take action. For someone else, they might feel happy and then take action to go out and meet clients, but for this specific person they aren’t taking action to meet clients, so they will need to try on different thoughts and find ones that do create actions and results.

What would you need to think and how would you need to feel to take consistent action and create the result of $5k a month? Take a guess at what thought might work, check in with your body to feel how it feels when you think that thought, and then check and see if that thought creates action for you.

C Goal: $5k a month

T

F

A take consistent action (write down what specific action you want to be taking)

R $5k a month

## Intentional model

**Date:** 2019-10-26  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I am having a hard time keeping a belief alive up to the inevitability. I work on it from impossibility to possibility. Sometimes I manage to reach the state inevitability and them I take some action but the belief reverse to possibility or impossibility quickly. How to keep an intentional belief alive?

C: Post on social media 
T: Facebook followers are friends and family . I don’t fully believe that people around me understand what is coaching and that they are ready to pay for coaching right now. 
F: Afraid to be pushy A: Posting just one video per week . 
R: 4 consults since April 
Note : 2 weeks ago I worked for one week on my belief to make 2K TODAY . I managed to reach full believe that it was possible that someone close to me was suffering and didn’t know I could help them. I signed 2 clients out of this message but I can’t seem to keep the belief alive. It goes away and then I am back to thinking that the rest of the people around me don’t know the value of coaching.

I want to work on the believe that people are waiting for me, they know the value of coaching, they want to hear from me and want to pay me right now . This feeling is there only few moments per week but it is fragile and shaky. I don’t know how to keep it alive in order to take massive action and make offer.

**ANSWER:**

Wouldn’t it be great if we just always believed that we could make 2k today the first time we thought it?

It’s like learning to ride a bike. There will be moments when it feels easy and natural, and then moments when you fall off the bike and have to start again. That’s how it goes. One day, after enough practice, you can ride a bike without thinking that you’ll fall off.

Don’t tell yourself that something has gone wrong. You are doing great. You are seeing that when you really belief you create the results you want. Keep working that belief and it will get less and less shaky.

Did you check out the podcast on the Belief Plan? It’s episode 19. It’s all about creating a plan to practice your belief.

C 2 years in business; not full time

Then, look at those thoughts and pick out a few to put into a model. Those are the thoughts causing the frustration, which you said isn’t serving you and is holding you back.

**Date:** 2019-10-27

I have gone out and met people at events and started a stream of FB lives and have no problem telling people I don’t know I am a coach and talking about my value. BUT when it comes to people I do know, I have a feeling of insecurity because my thought is that they won’t think I am qualified to do this work. I help moms with young children to reduce their stress and anxiety (I used to be an extremely anxious kid/person) and they only see me as the person I used to be not as who I am today. I know this feeling is getting in my way of making offers.

I want to be totally confident in the value of my coaching with ANYONE I talk to, not just strangers who don’t know the person I used to be. I started as wanting to be a health coach and I had no problem telling my family that because I have lost a lot of weight and kept it off easily for years so they could actually see that change. When I decided I want to help moms with young children with stress and anxiety that change in me isn’t something they see as readily, so I am not as confident in talking about it. I want to be the person that isn’t affected by what they say to me, which I am working on but I just haven’t been able to get there yet. I want to make offers to help people without caring what my family would think.

**ANSWER:**

Let’s look at the model you have right now.

C people I know

T They won’t think I am qualified to do this work.

F insecure

A

R

Let’s go right to that place that you are worried about. That place where someone you know doesn’t think you are qualified to help moms with stress and anxiety.

C someone I know tells me “You aren’t qualified to help moms with stress and anxiety.”

T

F

A

R

Fill in the rest of this model. What do you think when someone says that? That’s going to reveal your thoughts about yourself. It’s the thought that’s keeping you from not feeling confident. Look at that thought, how it’s affecting you, and then decide if you want to keep it.

Also, you are thinking that thought “My coaching will change women’s lives forever” could make you feel confident but it’s not. So what could you think that would actually produce the feeling of confidence? It can be just a small step toward that thought. A thought that feels slightly more confident than how you feel right now.

## Insecurity

**Date:** 2019-10-27  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I’m struggling with insecurity when it comes to what I have to offer. My insecurity is so bad that I struggle to offer free services to gain confidence. If I can’t offer a free service to flesh out my first program, how in the world am I going to get paid. I’m putting myself out there but my thoughts are definitely getting in the way.

I want to take action despite the inner negativity in my head. I’m signing up for networking events but when it comes to making an offer, the words just don’t come out. I even had someone say, wow I could use help in that area. I want to be willing to fail more.

**ANSWER:**

Feeling insecure doesn’t come from being bad at something and confidence doesn’t come from being good at something. Both feelings come from what we are thinking.

What are you thinking that’s causing you to feel insecure?

C at networking event

T

F insecure

A I go to make an offer and the words don’t come out. I don’t offer free services.

R I don’t make offers.

Changing your thoughts is how you change how you feel.

Is there something in your life you find really easy to sell? Something that you think everyone should try or own or have? Use those same thoughts and that same energy when you think about your free program. What makes it great? Why should people take it? Then, practice those thoughts on purpose.

## Production overtime

**Date:** 2019-10-27  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

How to make weekly podcasts inevitable. Here’s my current reality: 
C: I deliver value weekly through my podcasts. 
T: This is important, I love this topic, my peeps need this topic, I have a lot to say! A lot to bring, deliver, add, etc.. 
F: Excited, jazzed, motivated 
A: I start producing the podcast: Objectives, outline, add thoughts/content to outline, prep examples, research more detail, refine, etc, blah blah blah 
R: 3-8 WEEKS GO BY…still no podcast gets recorded or delivered!

One podcast delivered weekly. I invest four hours MAX to the prep, record and deliver.

**ANSWER:**

What’s happening is that there are two different models here. The thoughts and feelings in your model aren’t lining up with the actions you are taking and the result you are getting. You have some thoughts that are creating the actions of starting on the podcast, but then other thoughts come up that cause you to not finish it.

What thoughts and feelings are coming up for you that are causing you to not finish the podcast? Common thoughts are: It’s not good enough. I’m not good enough. It’s not done yet. Someone won’t like this. Someone might disagree. I have to make it perfect.

C Goal: one podcast delivered weekly

T

F

A not finishing the podcast (you can list exactly what you do instead of finishing it, do you just keep working on it? Do you do something else?)

R podcast not finished

Then, think about what you would really have to think to produce a podcast each week. What thoughts make you feel compelled to finish the podcast and hit publish?

C Goal: one podcast delivered weekly

T

F

A I make an outline in 30 minutes. I turn on my podcast recording equipment. I hit record. I talk. I stop recoding. I publish podcast. (these are example actions. You want to get specific about what needs to get done to complete the podcast)

R one podcast delivered this week

T

F

A

## Giving up

**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I’d like to be coached on the thought “If I don’t start to make money soon I should quit coaching.” I quit my job 18 months ago to regroup and get certified in coaching. I launched my business in January of this year and have made $2300.

I want to earn $5,000 a month but right now I really don’t believe this is possible.

**ANSWER:**

C Made $2300 in my business this year.

T If I don’t start to make money soon I should quit coaching.

F

A

R

Aren’t our minds funny sometimes? You have made money in your business – $2300 this year, but your mind thinks the thought “If I don’t start to make money soon I should quit coaching.” You HAVE made money – $2300.

I want you to fill in the rest of that model. How do you feel when you think the thought “If I don’t start to make money soon I should quit coaching”? What do you do when you feel that way? And what result do you get?

You said that you want to earn $5000 a month but that you don’t really believe it is possible. What comes up for you when you think about it? What thoughts are making if feel like it’s not possible? Those thoughts are the obstacles or the pathway to making $5000 a month. Instead of just thinking that you should believe it now, take a look at what thoughts are causing you to not believe that you can make $5000 a month and come back here for coaching on them.

## Investing in my business

**Date:** 2019-10-28  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I am putting pressure on myself about investing money into my business. From the beginning I didn’t want to use our personal family money for my business. However, it feels like a lot more money is going out then coming in and I can feel myself worrying about it. My husband is supportive of investing into my business, but I know how much he stresses about finances.

Ideally, I continue to use my own revenue to support my business. I have put 100% of my business income back into my business. I would not like to use our family money and have better thoughts about investing into my business.

**ANSWER:**

C money

T There is a lot more money going out than coming in.

F worry

A I wonder if I should use our family money for my business.

R

What is the result you get in this model? Is it a result you want to keep?

The exact amount of money coming in and going out would be a circumstance. For example I had $500 in revenue and $1000 in expenses this month. Then, you get to decide what you want to think about that. It’s the same for investing in your business.

C (exact money revenue and expense numbers)

T What do you want to think about the revenue and expenses?

F How do you feel when you think that?

A What do you do when you feel that way?

R What result do you get from those actions

It’s totally up to you do decide if you want to use your family money to invest in your business or not. Both are completely neutral.

Start by looking at your thoughts about why you don’t want to use family money to invest in your business and your thoughts about investing in your business. Do you like your reason for not using your family money to invest in your business?

A

## Disconnected

**Date:** 2019-10-28  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I struggle with actually knowing & connecting to the feeling in my body. (Wondering if I’m disconnected from my feelings). I always “think” the feeling not “feel” it. For example, the thought failure is necessary 
C my business 
T failure is necessary 
F ? Curious, (in reality I’m doubtful & not sure about this T) 
A fail evaluate learn grow 
R successful business

**ANSWER:**

It sounds like you are thinking that you should feel a certain way when you have a thought instead of looking to your body for the answer of how you actually feel.

How does it really feel when you think the thought, failure is necessary. Do you believe it? Does it feel uneasy? Allow it to be okay however you feel when you think that thought. It’s not bad if you don’t believe it yet, it’s just where you are at.

Also, don’t worry about finding the perfect word to describe how your body feels. Words are just common language that we use to communicate. It’s more important to check in with your body to see how you are feeling than to find the right word for it.

## Feeling salesy

**Date:** 2019-10-28  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I feel gross and salesy asking how committed people are on a scale of 1-10. I have been asked this question on some pushy sales calls and it did not make me want to buy. It feels like a tactic to make people feel bad about themselves and pressure them to buy, and I cannot be in service in that mindset.

Feel comfortable and confident in consults and completely unattached.

**ANSWER:**

Asking the client how committed they are on a scale of 1-10 isn’t about trying to convince the client that they should be at 10 or that they should sign up with you at all. What the questions is about is helping the client understand what’s going on in their own mind. Do they really want to make a change? How committed are they to changing? What objections (thoughts) does their mind come up with when they think about creating the result they want?

It’s how you are thinking about the question that is creating the feeling of gross/salesy. What are you thinking that’s causing that feeling? What happens when you think that thought?

C asking client how committed they are on a scale of 1-10

T

F gross/salesy

A

R

You can also try using the question on yourself to show yourself how it can create more awareness. Start asking yourself things on a scale of 1-10. Do I want pizza for supper? 1-10 Do I want to want to go to bed or keep watching tv? 1-10. All this question does is help us create awareness around our thoughts and what we are really thinking. When you use this question that way for yourself, you will see how you can also use it to create awareness for your clients.

## Overcoming objections

**Date:** 2019-10-28  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I want to be coached on overcoming objections. I’ve tried setting up follow up calls, asking them deeper questions, the Target in a box outcome idea. I know it’s my own thinking that is blocking me. I’ve scripted out my responses and thoughts and used the model, but I’m still getting stuck here and know if I could break through this area I would have many more YES’s.

My ideal outcome would be 50% close rate. Right now it ranges 5-20%. I have plenty of consultations, usually 8-12 a week and averaging 25-40/month. I’d like to be able to overcome their objections so I can help them achieve the results they want.

**ANSWER:**

“I’d like to be able to overcome their objections 
so I can help them achieve the results they want
.”  Is this true for you?  It may be.  It may not be.  It might just sound good to you.  It might sound better, more virtuous perhaps, than “I’d like to be able to overcome their objections so I can make money.”

What objections are you struggling to overcome?  Get specific.  Is there a trend?  For example, for the consults that led to paid clients, did most of them have similar objections or was it a grab bag?

Clients will present you with three types of objections, right?  Money, time, or status.  Those aren’t the actual reasons they decide not to buy what you’re selling.  It’s that they lack belief in one of three things: you, themselves or coaching.

Figure out what your YES consults have in common with each other as well as what your NO consults have in common with each other.  Identify the objections you struggle to overcome the most.  Then figure out what your thoughts are about them.  What part of you believes those objections?  Find that out, and then coach yourself and get yourself coached on those Ts.

P.S.:  You said that your conversion rate is 5-20%.  Which is it?  5%?  20%?  It isn’t a range.  It’s a precise number.  This is just math.

X: the number of consults that produced paid clients

Y: the total number of consults you’ve done to date.

(X/Y) x 100 = conversion rate.

P.S.S.: Your average number of consults per month isn’t a range either.  If you want to measure your progress, get precise on your numbers.  Otherwise, all you’re really doing is guessing.

## No objections

**Date:** 2019-10-28  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

On consults, I’m having trouble getting to the true objection to overcome it. When I ask them how committed they are, they say 10 but don’t sign. I feel like it’s a mix of them being too polite to say no and me not going in deep enough.

I want to schedule at least 2 consults a week and be converting at 80% or above until I get to 20 clients.

**ANSWER:**

Stop whatever you’re doing.  Go directly to the 2k for 2k Portal.  Click on the Bonuses tab.  Find the 32 Day Reboot.  Scroll down.  Keep scrolling.  Locate the Day 20 video titled “Q&A Step Four of the Five Step Close: Figuring Out the Objection.  Watch it.  Take notes.

Don’t have a pen handy?  No worries.  Here are the cliff notes.

If you want a better, more truthful answer, you’ve got to ask better questions.

You’re probably asking: “On a scale of 1-10, how committed are you to creating this result for yourself?”

Try this instead: “On a scale of 1-10, how committed are you to creating this result for yourself?  A 10 being nothing is going to get in the way.  Not time, not money, not what anybody else thinks.”

Whatever there objection is, if they didn’t already have one, it will have just popped into their head.

If that still doesn’t work, and they still give you a 10.  From a place of curiosity, consider asking what’s kept them from already having that result.

Also, pay attention to your own thoughts about asking questions to find the objection.  Your own T about a client saying yes on the consult then not showing up for coaching is, in part, because you think they’re being “too polite.”  That may be true.  It may not be true.  That’s just your T.  With that said, ask yourself why you aren’t digging deeper on the call.  Do you feel like you’d be prying or being too pushy if you dug deeper and asked more questions?  Do you think that’s impolite?

## Circumstances

**Date:** 2019-10-28  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I sometimes can’t see the C if I have worked the model from the thought or the result.

I’d like to have more clarity of getting to the C if I’m not starting from the C.

**ANSWER:**

Don’t skip the C.  Start there.  Be specific, but keep it simple.  There’s always a C.  What fact were you thinking about that led to your brain creating the sentence in your mind–the thought–that you put in your T line?

Your brain can’t come up with a T without there being a C.  You don’t have to go in order down the Model, but don’t give in to temptation to skip the C line and go right to filling in the T or the R line.  The C is the basis of your model.

Refer back to the Making Money with the Model Workbook (pg. 4), which goes over Model Rules.  “You always have a C. Never fill out the model without a C.”  Why?  Because identifying the C is what allows you to see the neutrality of the circumstance and gain awareness about the contrast of your emotions when your brain thinks a thought about the neutral C.

P.S. With questions like this include the Model that you’re working on so you can get even more targeted coaching. 🙂

## Money triggers

**Date:** 2019-10-28  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I literally flinch, whenever money is mentioned on a consult. The client may mention (or just hint) about money being “tight” or being a problem and my heart races. “Money” triggers this very visceral reaction through my body when it is mentioned and I KNOW it is effecting my consults. This happens even BEFORE getting to any money objections, it could be just in the discovery stage of the call. Usually by the time we get to money objections, I am so anxious, it is hard for me to navigate them with ease (if at all).

I’d love to have a way of self-correcting this in the moment. Ideally, I would never like to be triggered by money again…but I know I need to work my way there…so, how can I self correct so I can calm any anxieties and still push through the money objections without letting my models about money get in the way…

**ANSWER:**

Look at how you talk about money!  Imagine if money were a person and overheard you saying all of those things.  Then you want money to show up and be there to support you.

Step 1: Stop blaming money for how you feel about it.

Step 2: Put “Money” in your C line and do a thought download about it.

Step 3: Figure out what in the world your thoughts are about money.

Step 4: Run a model on each one of the Ts you write down, so you can figure out what feeling comes up when you think each thought.

Step 5: Notice that it’s not Money that’s causing your feelings; it’s your thoughts about Money.

Here’s one example.

C: Money

T:

F: Anxious

A:

R:

You’re probably finding it difficult to deal with money objections on your consults, because you believe the same story about money as your potential clients.  In order to “self-correct” you need to gain some self-awareness on what you think about money and the results you create with that thinking.  Can you get the results you want with your current Ts?  What would you have to think instead?

## Bad coach

**Date:** 2019-10-28  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I just finished a 6mth business coaching relationship where I invested $10K and made back $2k. Only upon reflection did I realize that there were not good business boundaries. He was doing more purpose coaching. He offered to work with me every other week until I make back my investment. I realized this wouldn’t be good for me and I just asked to be able to call him when I needed him and not take me out of the mastermind group. He tried to upsell me on the mastermind and told me that only they would be getting his priority. I would like at least a partial refund, however, I’ve been paralyzed to ask. I feel like I am handicapped by this and not confident like I need this to happen so I can fully jump in to the 2k program.

My ideal outcome would be for me to be able to contact him from a place of power, tell him what I think actually went wrong in the coaching and ask for a refund or partial refund. Also, I would like to be clear about how much I am asking for. I want to go into this new coaching program with confidence.

**ANSWER:**

Grab a piece of paper.  Draw a line down the middle.  At the top, on the left side write “Fact” and on the right side write “Story.”  Look at the problem you requested to be coached on.  Separate the facts from the story you’re telling yourself about the six month program, the coach, the $10k you paid, the $2k you made, and the words the coach said to you. ALL of these things are completely neutral.  You’ve made yourself the victim in this story, blaming your results or, better yet, your lack of results on the six-month program, the coach, the lack of business boundaries, etc.  You’re blaming him and his actions for your own inaction as it relates to the 2k program.

You aren’t taking responsibility for the results you’ve created.  You’ve assigned that responsibility to people and programs outside of yourself.  Here’s the thing: Until you accept that you are the reason you don’t have the results you want, you won’t get the results you want.  Not in the six-month program or in 2k for 2k.

Why do you think you’re entitled to a partial refund?  What Ts come up for you in response to that question?  Write them down.  Question each one of them.  From a place of emotional adulthood, taking complete responsibility for yourself, the thoughts you chose to think during those six months, the feelings you chose to feel, and how you chose to show up during that time period (both your actions and your inactions), find all the ways that the Ts you listed aren’t true.  Is there a part of you that doesn’t believe you deserve a refund?  Is that T or something like it what’s causing you to feel paralyzed/

C: Ask for $[insert amount].

T:

F: Paralyzed.

A:

R:

Get honest with yourself.  You know how much money you’d like to get back.  By telling yourself you don’t, you get to indulge in indecision about what amount to ask for so you don’t have to move to the next step of actually asking for it.  What if you just decided on a number?  Is it all $10k you paid?  Is it $8k (the $10k you paid minus the $2k you made back).  Is it half of that?  Whatever the amount, why that number?  Was there a money-back guarantee?  Do you just believe you should have recouped what you invested?

Notice that all of the drama you’re experiencing about your previous program and your previous coach is caused by your thinking.  Also notice that by continuing to indulge in the drama you’re letting the $8k you didn’t make back during the last six months keep you from making $8k or $80k over the next six months.  Do you see how that’s true?  How that’s a choice that you’re making?

Last but not least, know this (like know it so deeply that this sinks into your bones): How you decide to feel and show up in 2k for 2k has absolutely nothing to do with whether or not you get a partial refund from your other coach.  You don’t “need” that to happen so you can “fully jump in.”  You can just decide to jump in and then do it.  It’s really that simple.

## Thoughts sparking FOMO

**Date:** 2019-10-28  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I realise that a lot of my unintentional models R = $0 and I continue to believe in these thoughts (imposter syndrome 99% of the time). So then I run intentional models and the ‘positive’ thoughts are actually sparking FOMO, overwhelm, uncertainty or I just straight up don’t believe them. I am trying to get out of this cycle by doing more models. I watched a video yesterday where you said “making money needs to be non-negotiable” like feeding a baby and I realise it’s not for me. Do I just keep doing models on this to get to the root thoughts??

A couple of ideal Rs: the belief that making money is non-negotiable, being financially independent, A top expert in my field, and probably my biggest struggle is just showing up as I am and being okay with me – vulnerability. (even applying for public coaching is feeling sooo vulnerable).

**ANSWER:**

Doing more models doesn’t seem to be working for you. It’s not getting you to a place where you are making more money.

C $0 in my business

T I need to do more models to feel better before I can make money.

F resigned

A Do more models, don’t take action in my business

R $0

Don’t think about thoughts as being positive or negative. Think about them as being useful or not useful. A useful thought is one that creates a feeling that motivates you to take some action in creating clients. A not useful thought is a $0 thought. It doesn’t matter if the thought sounds positive or negative or neutral to someone else. What matters is if that thought helps you take action.

What would you need to think to take action in your business? It’s not the thought I need to do more models. It might be “It’s possible that maybe one person out there might want what I have to offer.” You might need to start with a thought like that – a possibility thought. Try it on and see how that feels and what you do when you really believe that. Keep trying thoughts on until you find the ones that create action for you.

My biggest question right now is what process should I use to coach clients? I am an Ayurvedic Health Counselor (Indian traditional medicine), reiki practitioner, yoga teacher and life coach (in training). C: Coaching

T: I am not being very clear on teaching the frameworks.

F:

A:

R:

Your clients’ transformations are not created by the “frameworks.”  They create their transformations by changing their mindsets by coaching with you.  Instead of thinking that you’re underdelivering, you could focus your thinking on all of the ways you overdeliver, because of the various “frameworks” you’ve studied.  You can choose to think of all of the ways your clients benefit from the array of tools you have from different coaching philosophies.  Did you know that there are life coaches out there who don’t even know about The Model?!  Crazy, right?  The Model is just another tool or “framework” that coaches can use to coach, no different from the “frameworks” you incorporate into your own coaching practice.

seem
 like a legitimate dilemma.  What if it’s just an excuse?

## Struggle to share on social media

**Date:** 2019-10-28

I am really struggling with offering my content through social media. In my head I know I bring excellent value and that I can deliver. I’ve tried to model it over and over. I want to serve and give people a chance to change their lives through FB so bad I could just spit! (So, yeah, I want it bad.) What am I afraid of? Please help.

I want to powerfully show up being my true self on Facebook posts offering value and inviting people in. Into my world. Into my belief. Into my transformation. So they can have it for themselves.

**ANSWER:**

What are you afraid of?

Answer that question yourself.  Write it all down.  Everything that comes up is just a thought.  Thoughts are just sentences in our minds.  They aren’t true.  Run models on each thought.  See the results (or the lack thereof) you create (or don’t) when you’re thinking those thoughts.

C: Social media

T: I am afraid of . . .

F:

A:

R:

Also, notice how your thinking (specifically your T “I know I bring excellent value and that I can deliver) doesn’t match up with your inaction.  One of two things is true: Either you’re telling yourself “I know I bring excellent value and that I can deliver” but you don’t actually believe that to be true, OR, you believe it, but care more about what other people think about what you have to say than you care about what you think about you have to say, and you trust their opinions over your own.  Which is it?

## How to allow feelings

**Date:** 2019-10-28  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I’ve seen that my feelings are generating actions and results I don’t want. How do I allow – rather than resist – feelings I don’t want while at the same time creating feelings and thoughts I do want? Sometimes feel stuck here.

I want to know how to allow feelings and also generate new productive ones so I can generate what I want to achieve – more clients.

**ANSWER:**

Learning to allow an emotion is a skill you have to build just like you build a muscle.  You build it through practice.  We resist emotions because, ultimately, we are resisting/avoiding discomfort.  In part because we think we shouldn’t feel negative emotions.  How do we know this isn’t true?  Because we feel them anyways, whether we like it or not.  The funny thing is…by resisting or avoiding emotions we only end up elongating the amount of time we feel them.

Here are a few ways to practice allowing emotions.  First, start by recognizing that emotions are just vibrations in your body.  Think of the absolute worst, most excruciating emotion you can think of.  Maybe it’s shame or humiliation.  Whatever it is, describe how that emotion feels in your body when you feel it.  Get specific.  Write it all down.  What does it feel like in your body?  Where in your body do you feel it?  What makes it different from another negative emotion like heartbreak or sorrow or overwhelm or stress or anger?

Often times, with negative emotions, you’ll will feel your body tighten in some way.  It’s the “resistance” muscle you’ve built up to brace against negative feelings.  Instead of trying to toss away the negative ones like a hot potato, just notice them and breathe them in.  Each feeling is just a vibration.  It can’t actually hurt you.  Practicing allowing negative emotions until you start to recognize that–that they can’t actually harm you.  It isn’t possible.  Whatever the negative emotion, you can handle it.  It will pass.  You’ll be there on the other side of it when it does.  Think of yourself as a train and emotions as passengers.  Passengers get on and get off…the train keeps going.  It doesn’t stop.  Neither will you.

Different ways to share your story include sharing the following:

The changes you’re making and why.

Client success stories

F: Disconnected/Frustrated/Stuck

A:

R: Shared nothing.

## Discouraged

**Date:** 2019-10-28  
**Topics:** archive-2019-2020

**QUESTION:**

I haven’t generated a single consult from meeting people and making offers. I feel discouraged. I’ve listened to Stacey talk about choosing to believe it’s working and to just keep going and meeting as many people as possible. telling them you are a life coach, and making offers, but I clearly don’t believe because I feel discouraged.

I would like a stronger belief. I’d like to be moving into inevitability belief.

**ANSWER:**

You want to go straight from impossibility to inevitability, but you haven’t even passed through possibility yet.

Think about these thoughts/beliefs.  On a scale of 1-10, how much do you believe each one?

Stacey can get consults by meeting people and telling them she’s a coach.

Coaches can get consults by meeting people and telling them their a coach.

I can get consults by meeting people and telling them their a coach.

The process works.

The process works for Stacey.

The process works for some coaches.

The process works for all coaches.

The process works for me.

The process doesn’t work for me.

The process doesn’t work.

You can’t just believe in the process generally.  You have to believe it as it applies to you.  You have to get to possibility and then probability before you get to inevitability.  For every one of those beliefs, ask yourself why you gave it the number you did.

How many networking events have you attended?  How many people have you met?  How many offers have you made?  How many networking events are you willing to attend in order to be successful?  What if success was guaranteed?  How would you show up differently?

Right now you said that you’re discouraged.  Notice how you’re going to show up at events, when meeting people or when making offers when you’re feeling discouraged.

C: Making offers.

T: I haven’t generated a single consult.

F: Discouraged.

A: [Fill in the As you take/don’t take]

R: Zero consults.

Who wants to work with a discouraged life coach?  You want to show up to those events feeling confident and inspired.  (Listen to Episode 17 of the Make Money as a Life Coach Podcast “How to Get Consults” all about this specific topic.)
